+/**
+ * 07-phrase-catalog.js
+ *
+ * Phrase catalog loading, formatting helpers, and analyst/developer logic generation for standardized audit language.
+ * Auto-generated documentation comments explain function purpose, expected inputs, and module sections without changing runtime behavior.
+ */
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Phrase catalog state
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+const REQUIRED_CATALOG_KEYS = ['version', 'description', 'auto_note_prefixes', 'notes', 'format_field', 'list_format', 'developer_logic', 'analyst_logic'];
+let phraseCatalog = {};
+const missingPhrasePaths = new Set();
+const catalogLoadWarnings = [];
+
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Catalog loading and lookup
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/**
+ * Loads and validates the phrase catalog JSON.
+ */
+async function initPhraseCatalog() {
+  missingPhrasePaths.clear();
+  catalogLoadWarnings.length = 0;
+  try {
+    const response = await fetch('logic_phrase_catalog.json', { cache: 'no-store' });
+    if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`${response.status} ${response.statusText}`);
+    const json = await response.json();
+    validateCatalogShape(json);
+    phraseCatalog = json;
+    status('Phrase catalog loaded from logic_phrase_catalog.json.', 'ok');
+  } catch (error) {
+    phraseCatalog = {};
+    catalogLoadWarnings.push(`Phrase catalog could not be loaded: ${error.message}`);
+    status('Phrase catalog could not be loaded. Analyst Logic will show missing phrase warnings.', 'bad');
+  }
+}
+
+/**
+ * Checks that the phrase catalog has required top-level keys.
+ * @param {any} catalog - Input used by validateCatalogShape.
+ */
+function validateCatalogShape(catalog) {
+  if (!catalog || typeof catalog !== 'object' || Array.isArray(catalog)) throw new Error('logic_phrase_catalog.json must contain a JSON object.');
+  const missing = REQUIRED_CATALOG_KEYS.filter((key) => !(key in catalog));
+  if (missing.length) throw new Error(`logic_phrase_catalog.json is missing required top-level key(s): ${missing.join(', ')}.`);
+}
+
+/**
+ * Reads a nested value from the phrase catalog.
+ * @param {any} path - Catalog path or file path.
+ * @param {any} defaultValue - Fallback value when a catalog path is missing.
+ */
+function getCatalogValue(path, defaultValue = undefined) {
+  if (!path) return defaultValue;
+  let current = phraseCatalog;
+  for (const part of String(path).split('.')) {
+    if (current && typeof current === 'object' && Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(current, part)) current = current[part];
+    else {
+      missingPhrasePaths.add(path);
+      return defaultValue;
+    }
+  }
+  return current;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Reads a string template from the phrase catalog.
+ * @param {any} path - Catalog path or file path.
+ */
+function phraseTemplate(path) {
+  const value = getCatalogValue(path, undefined);
+  if (typeof value !== 'string') {
+    missingPhrasePaths.add(path);
+    return '';
+  }
+  return value;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Substitutes template placeholders with values.
+ * @param {any} template - Input used by renderPhraseFromTemplate.
+ * @param {any} values - Input used by renderPhraseFromTemplate.
+ */
+function renderPhraseFromTemplate(template, values = {}) {
+  return String(template || '').replace(/\{([A-Za-z0-9_.-]+)\}/g, (match, key) => {
+    if (Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(values, key)) return String(values[key] ?? '');
+    return match;
+  });
+}
+
+/**
+ * Renders a phrase template by catalog path.
+ * @param {any} path - Catalog path or file path.
+ * @param {any} values - Input used by renderCatalogPhrase.
+ */
+function renderCatalogPhrase(path, values = {}) {
+  const template = phraseTemplate(path);
+  if (!template) return `[Missing phrase path: ${path}]`;
+  return renderPhraseFromTemplate(template, values);
+}
+
+/**
+ * Reads an array from the phrase catalog with fallback.
+ * @param {any} path - Catalog path or file path.
+ * @param {any} fallback - Fallback value when lookup fails.
+ */
+function catalogArray(path, fallback = []) {
+  const value = getCatalogValue(path, undefined);
+  return Array.isArray(value) ? value : fallback;
+}
+
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Phrase formatting helpers
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/**
+ * Quotes a value for standardized analyst logic.
+ * @param {any} value - Value to process or assign.
+ */
+function quotePhraseValue(value) {
+  const clean = String(value ?? '').trim();
+  return clean ? `"${clean}"` : '""';
+}
+
+/**
+ * Resolves a source alias from audit, repo, or workbook metadata.
+ * @param {any} sourceFile - Raw source file name.
+ */
+function sourceAliasFor(sourceFile) {
+  const fromState = (state.audit.sources || []).find((source) => source.name === sourceFile)?.alias;
+  if (fromState) return fromState;
+  const fromRepo = (repoLibraryState?.sourceAliases || []).find((item) => item.sourceFile === sourceFile)?.alias;
+  if (fromRepo) return fromRepo;
+  const fromWorkbook = (typeof workbookState !== 'undefined' ? workbookState.sourceAliases || [] : []).find((item) => item.sourceFile === sourceFile)?.alias;
+  return fromWorkbook || sourceFile || '';
+}
+
+/**
+ * Formats a source/field pair as standardized column text.
+ * @param {any} sourceFile - Raw source file name.
+ * @param {any} alias - Input used by tableColumnPlain.
+ * @param {any} fieldName - Raw field name.
+ * @param {any} omitSource - Input used by tableColumnPlain.
+ */
+function tableColumnPlain(sourceFile, alias, fieldName, omitSource = false) {
+  const field = String(fieldName || '').trim();
+  if (!field) return '';
+  const source = String(sourceFile || '').trim();
+  const resolvedAlias = String(alias || sourceAliasFor(source) || source).trim();
+  if (source === 'First Available Value' || resolvedAlias === 'First Available Value') return field;
+  if (source === 'Calculated Field' || resolvedAlias === 'Calculated Field' || source === 'Created Columns') return `new column ${quotePhraseValue(field)}`;
+  if (omitSource || !resolvedAlias) return `column ${quotePhraseValue(field)}`;
+  return `file ${quotePhraseValue(resolvedAlias)} column ${quotePhraseValue(field)}`;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Formats a list of comparison values using catalog list templates.
+ * @param {any} value - Value to process or assign.
+ */
+function formattedValueList(value) {
+  const values = Array.isArray(value) ? value : String(value ?? '').split(/\r?\n/).map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean);
+  const quoted = values.map(quotePhraseValue);
+  if (quoted.length === 0) return '';
+  if (quoted.length === 1) return quoted[0];
+  if (quoted.length === 2) return renderCatalogPhrase('list_format.two_item_template', { first: quoted[0], second: quoted[1] });
+  return renderCatalogPhrase('list_format.many_item_template', { items: quoted.slice(0, -1).join(', '), last: quoted.at(-1) });
+}
+
+
+function conditionCompareMathOperationsForPhrase(condition = {}) {
+  if (typeof parseSourceMathOperations !== 'function') return [];
+  return parseSourceMathOperations(condition.compareMathJson || condition.compare_math_json || condition.compareMath || []);
+}
+
+function compareMathOperatorWord(operator = '') {
+  if (operator === '+') return 'plus';
+  if (operator === '-') return 'minus';
+  if (operator === '*') return 'times';
+  if (operator === '/') return 'divided by';
+  return '';
+}
+
+function compareMathOperandPlain(operation = {}, fallbackSourceFile = '') {
+  if ((operation.operandType || 'number') === 'field') {
+    const sourceFile = operation.operandSourceFile || fallbackSourceFile;
+    const alias = operation.operandSourceAlias || sourceAliasFor(sourceFile);
+    return tableColumnPlain(sourceFile, alias, operation.operandFieldName || '');
+  }
+  return String(operation.operand || '').trim();
+}
+
+function conditionCompareFieldPlain(condition = {}) {
+  const base = tableColumnPlain(
+    condition.compareSourceFile || condition.sourceFile,
+    condition.compareSourceAlias || condition.sourceAlias,
+    condition.compareFieldName
+  );
+  const operations = conditionCompareMathOperationsForPhrase(condition);
+  const mathText = operations.map((operation) => {
+    const word = compareMathOperatorWord(operation.operator);
+    const operand = compareMathOperandPlain(operation, condition.compareSourceFile || condition.sourceFile);
+    return word && operand ? `${word} ${operand}` : '';
+  }).filter(Boolean).join(' ');
+  return `${base}${mathText ? ` ${mathText}` : ''}`.trim();
+}
+
+/**
+ * Formats the right side of a condition.
+ * @param {any} condition - Condition object to format or render.
+ */
+function conditionValuePlain(condition) {
+  if (condition.valueMode === 'blank') return 'blank/null';
+  if (condition.valueMode === 'field') return conditionCompareFieldPlain(condition);
+  if (condition.valueMode === 'expression') return String(condition.expression || '').trim();
+  return formattedValueList(condition.compareValue || condition.value || '');
+}
+
+/**
+ * Formats a full condition as plain standardized text.
+ * @param {any} condition - Condition object to format or render.
+ * @param {any} omitSource - Input used by conditionToPlain.
+ */
+function conditionToPlain(condition, omitSource = false) {
+  const left = tableColumnPlain(condition.sourceFile, condition.sourceAlias, condition.fieldName || condition.field, omitSource);
+  const operator = condition.valueMode === 'blank'
+    ? (String(condition.operator || '').includes('not') ? 'is not blank/null' : 'is blank/null')
+    : (condition.operator || 'is equal to');
+  const value = condition.valueMode === 'blank' ? '' : conditionValuePlain(condition);
+  return `${left} ${operator}${value ? ` ${value}` : ''}`.trim();
+}
+
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Condition normalization and analyst logic
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/**
+ * Normalizes stored condition shapes into guided condition objects.
+ * @param {any} step - Logic step object being read or modified.
+ */
+function parseStepConditions(step) {
+  const params = step.params || {};
+  if (params.conditions_json) {
+    try {
+      const parsed = JSON.parse(params.conditions_json);
+      if (Array.isArray(parsed)) return parsed;
+    } catch {}
+  }
+  return (step.conditions || []).map((condition, index) => ({
+    id: condition.id || uid('cond'),
+    connector: index === 0 ? 'IF' : (condition.connector || 'AND'),
+    sourceFile: condition.sourceFile || step.source || params.source_file || '',
+    sourceAlias: condition.sourceAlias || params.source_alias || sourceAliasFor(condition.sourceFile || step.source || params.source_file || ''),
+    fieldName: condition.fieldName || condition.field || params.field_name || '',
+    operator: condition.operator || params.operator || 'is equal to',
+    valueMode: condition.valueMode || 'values',
+    compareValue: condition.compareValue || condition.value || params.compare_value || '',
+    compareSourceFile: condition.compareSourceFile || '',
+    compareSourceAlias: condition.compareSourceAlias || '',
+    compareFieldName: condition.compareFieldName || '',
+    expression: condition.expression || '',
+    compareMathJson: condition.compareMathJson || condition.compare_math_json || '',
+  }));
+}
+
+const INVERSE_CONDITION_OPERATOR_MAP = {
+  'is equal to': 'is not equal to',
+  'is not equal to': 'is equal to',
+  'contains': 'does not contain',
+  'does not contain': 'contains',
+  'starts with': 'does not start with',
+  'does not start with': 'starts with',
+  'ends with': 'does not end with',
+  'does not end with': 'ends with',
+  'is greater than': 'is less than or equal to',
+  'is greater than or equal to': 'is less than',
+  'is less than': 'is greater than or equal to',
+  'is less than or equal to': 'is greater than',
+  'is blank/null': 'is not blank/null',
+  'is not blank/null': 'is blank/null',
+  'matches expression': 'does not match expression',
+  'does not match expression': 'matches expression',
+};
+
+/**
+ * Returns the logical opposite of a guided IF operator.
+ * @param {string} operator - Operator label from the IF condition editor.
+ * @param {string} valueMode - Condition value mode, used for blank/null defaults.
+ */
+function inverseConditionOperator(operator, valueMode = 'values') {
+  const normalized = String(operator || '').trim().toLowerCase();
+  if (INVERSE_CONDITION_OPERATOR_MAP[normalized]) return INVERSE_CONDITION_OPERATOR_MAP[normalized];
+  if (valueMode === 'blank') return normalized.includes('not') ? 'is blank/null' : 'is not blank/null';
+  if (normalized.startsWith('does not ')) return normalized.replace(/^does not\s+/, '');
+  if (normalized.startsWith('does ')) return normalized.replace(/^does\s+/, 'does not ');
+  if (normalized.startsWith('is not ')) return normalized.replace(/^is not\s+/, 'is ');
+  if (normalized.startsWith('is ')) return normalized.replace(/^is\s+/, 'is not ');
+  // Unknown operators are left unchanged because the app cannot infer a safe inverse.
+  return operator || 'is equal to';
+}
+
+/**
+ * Returns the De Morgan opposite of a condition connector.
+ * @param {string} connector - Connector from a guided condition row.
+ */
+function inverseConditionConnector(connector) {
+  const normalized = String(connector || '').trim().toUpperCase();
+  if (normalized === 'AND') return 'OR';
+  if (normalized === 'OR') return 'AND';
+  return 'AND';
+}
+
+/**
+ * Creates the inverse of one guided condition while preserving source/value metadata.
+ * @param {any} condition - Guided condition object from parseStepConditions.
+ * @param {number} index - Zero-based condition index.
+ */
+function invertGuidedCondition(condition, index = 0) {
+  const next = {
+    ...condition,
+    id: uid('cond'),
+    connector: index === 0 ? 'IF' : inverseConditionConnector(condition.connector),
+    operator: inverseConditionOperator(condition.operator, condition.valueMode),
+  };
+  if ((next.valueMode || 'values') === 'blank') {
+    next.compareValue = '';
+    next.compareFieldName = '';
+    next.expression = '';
+  }
+  return next;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Builds inverted conditions for a complete IF step.
+ * @param {any} step - IF step to invert.
+ */
+function invertedConditionsForStep(step) {
+  const parsed = parseStepConditions(step);
+  const conditions = parsed.length ? parsed : (typeof defaultGuidedCondition === 'function' ? [defaultGuidedCondition(step)] : []);
+  return conditions.map((condition, index) => invertGuidedCondition(condition, index));
+}
+
+/**
+ * Formats all IF/THEN conditions as a condition group.
+ * @param {any} step - Logic step object being read or modified.
+ */
+function conditionGroupPlain(step) {
+  return parseStepConditions(step).map((condition, index) => {
+    const connector = index === 0 ? 'IF' : (condition.connector || 'AND');
+    return renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.if_then.condition_line', { connector, condition_text: conditionToPlain(condition, false) });
+  }).join(' ').trim();
+}
+
+
+/**
+ * Ensures condition text starts with an IF-style prefix.
+ * @param {any} text - Input used by ensureIfThenPrefix.
+ */
+function ensureIfThenPrefix(text) {
+  const value = String(text || '').trim();
+  if (!value) return value;
+  if (/^(IF|ELSE IF|ELSE)\b/i.test(value)) return value;
+  return `IF ${value}`;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Formats the THEN action for an IF/THEN step.
+ * @param {any} step - Logic step object being read or modified.
+ */
+function thenActionPlain(step) {
+  const params = step.params || {};
+  const actionType = params.then_action_type || 'freeform';
+  if (actionType === 'display_text') return renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.if_then.then_display_text', { message: quotePhraseValue(params.then_text || ''), output_column: quotePhraseValue(params.then_output_column || 'Error Message') });
+  if (actionType === 'set_column_value') return renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.if_then.then_set_column_value', { target_column: quotePhraseValue(params.then_target_column || ''), value_column: tableColumnPlain(params.then_value_source_file || params.source_file, params.then_value_source_alias || params.source_alias, params.then_value_field || '') });
+  if (actionType === 'do_not_display') return renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.if_then.then_do_not_display', {});
+  return renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.if_then.then_freeform', { action: params.then_action || step.action || '' });
+}
+
+/**
+ * Formats an ELSE step action.
+ * @param {any} step - Logic step object being read or modified.
+ */
+function elseActionPlain(step) {
+  const params = step.params || {};
+  const actionType = params.then_action_type || 'freeform';
+  if (actionType === 'display_text') return renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.else.display_text', { message: quotePhraseValue(params.then_text || ''), output_column: quotePhraseValue(params.then_output_column || 'Error Message') });
+  if (actionType === 'set_column_value') return renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.else.set_column_value', { target_column: quotePhraseValue(params.then_target_column || ''), value_column: tableColumnPlain(params.then_value_source_file || params.source_file, params.then_value_source_alias || params.source_alias, params.then_value_field || '') });
+  if (actionType === 'do_not_display') return renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.else.do_not_display', {});
+  if (params.then_action) return renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.else.freeform', { action: params.then_action });
+  return renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.else.default', {});
+}
+
+/**
+ * Selects the phrase path for a Create Column step.
+ * @param {any} params - Step params object.
+ */
+function createColumnPhrasePath(params) {
+  if (params.value_mode === 'Static value') return 'analyst_logic.create_column.static_value';
+  if (params.value_mode === 'Source field value') return 'analyst_logic.create_column.source_field';
+  if (params.value_mode === 'Concatenate fields') return 'analyst_logic.create_column.concatenate_fields';
+  if (params.expression_type === 'first_characters') return 'analyst_logic.create_column.first_characters';
+  if (params.expression_type === 'character_count') return 'analyst_logic.create_column.character_count';
+  if (params.value_mode === 'Expression / calculation') return 'analyst_logic.create_column.expression';
+  return 'analyst_logic.create_column.default';
+}
+
+/**
+ * Returns true when a join type represents a Union.
+ * @param {any} joinType - Input used by phraseIsUnionJoinType.
+ */
+function phraseIsUnionJoinType(joinType) { return String(joinType || '').trim().toUpperCase() === 'UNION'; }
+
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Create Column source-field math and rounding phrase helpers
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/**
+ * Formats the rounding precision target for analyst-facing Create Column text.
+ * @param {any} decimalPlacesValue - Raw decimal places value.
+ */
+function atlasRoundingTargetText(decimalPlacesValue) {
+  const decimals = Number.parseInt(decimalPlacesValue, 10);
+  const safeDecimals = Number.isFinite(decimals) && decimals >= 0 ? decimals : 0;
+  if (safeDecimals === 0) return 'nearest whole number';
+  return `${safeDecimals} decimal place${safeDecimals === 1 ? '' : 's'}`;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Formats the rounding direction phrase for analyst-facing Create Column text.
+ * @param {string} roundingMode - Rounding mode.
+ */
+function atlasRoundingDirectionText(roundingMode) {
+  if (roundingMode === 'down') return 'down to';
+  if (roundingMode === 'up') return 'up to';
+  return 'to';
+}
+
+function atlasReadableSourceFieldName(p = {}) {
+  return quotePhraseValue?.(p.field_name || '') || `"${esc(p.field_name || '')}"`;
+}
+
+function atlasSourceMathOperationPhrase(operation, index, sourceField) {
+  const operand = String(operation?.operand || '').trim();
+  if (!operand) return '';
+  if (index === 0) {
+    if (operation.operator === '+') return `adding ${operand} to column ${sourceField}`;
+    if (operation.operator === '-') return `subtracting ${operand} from column ${sourceField}`;
+    if (operation.operator === '*') return `multiplying column ${sourceField} by ${operand}`;
+    if (operation.operator === '/') return `dividing column ${sourceField} by ${operand}`;
+    return '';
+  }
+  if (operation.operator === '+') return `adding ${operand}`;
+  if (operation.operator === '-') return `subtracting ${operand}`;
+  if (operation.operator === '*') return `multiplying the result by ${operand}`;
+  if (operation.operator === '/') return `dividing by ${operand}`;
+  return '';
+}
+
+function atlasJoinReadableList(items = []) {
+  const filtered = items.filter(Boolean);
+  if (!filtered.length) return '';
+  if (filtered.length === 1) return filtered[0];
+  if (filtered.length === 2) return `${filtered[0]} and ${filtered[1]}`;
+  return `${filtered.slice(0, -1).join(', ')}, and ${filtered.at(-1)}`;
+}
+
+function atlasSourceFieldMathPhrase(p = {}) {
+  const sourceField = atlasReadableSourceFieldName(p);
+  const operations = typeof parseSourceMathOperations === 'function' ? parseSourceMathOperations(p) : [];
+  return atlasJoinReadableList(operations.map((operation, index) => atlasSourceMathOperationPhrase(operation, index, sourceField)));
+}
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Step-to-analyst-logic renderer
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/**
+ * Generates standardized analyst-facing logic text for a step.
+ * @param {any} step - Logic step object being read or modified.
+ */
+function analystLogic(step) {
+  normalizeCatalogStep(step);
+  const params = step.params || {};
+  const stepType = step.step_type;
+  if (stepType === 'reference_source') {
+    const sourceFile = params.source_file || step.source || '';
+    const alias = params.source_alias || sourceAliasFor(sourceFile);
+    return alias && alias !== sourceFile
+      ? renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.reference_source.with_alias', { source_file: quotePhraseValue(sourceFile), source_alias: quotePhraseValue(alias) })
+      : renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.reference_source.without_alias', { source_file: quotePhraseValue(sourceFile) });
+  }
+  if (stepType === 'create_column') {
+    const columnName = params.column_name || step.field || '';
+    if ((params.value_mode === 'Static value' || !params.value_mode) && !String(params.static_value || '').trim()) {
+      return `Create new blank column ${quotePhraseValue(columnName)}.`;
+    }
+    const tableColumn = tableColumnPlain(params.source_file, params.source_alias, params.field_name);
+    return renderCatalogPhrase(createColumnPhrasePath(params), {
+      column_name: quotePhraseValue(columnName),
+      static_value: quotePhraseValue(params.static_value || ''),
+      table_column: tableColumn,
+      expression: params.expression || '',
+      character_count: params.character_count || '',
+      first_column: tableColumnPlain(params.concat_source_1 || params.source_file, sourceAliasFor(params.concat_source_1 || params.source_file), params.concat_field_1),
+      second_column: tableColumnPlain(params.concat_source_2 || params.source_file, sourceAliasFor(params.concat_source_2 || params.source_file), params.concat_field_2),
+    });
+  }
+  if (stepType === 'if_then') {
+    const group = ensureIfThenPrefix(conditionGroupPlain(step));
+    const action = thenActionPlain(step);
+    const phrase = action
+      ? renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.if_then.condition_group_with_action', { condition_group: group, then_action: action })
+      : renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.if_then.condition_group_without_action', { condition_group: group });
+    return ensureIfThenPrefix(phrase);
+  }
+  if (stepType === 'else') return elseActionPlain(step);
+  if (stepType === 'join') {
+    if (phraseIsUnionJoinType(params.join_type)) return renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.union_sources.default', { left_source: quotePhraseValue(params.left_source || params.union_left_source || ''), right_source: quotePhraseValue(params.right_source || params.union_right_source || ''), output_source: quotePhraseValue(params.output_source || step.source || '') });
+    return renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.join.default', { left_column: tableColumnPlain(params.left_source, params.left_alias, params.left_field), right_column: tableColumnPlain(params.right_source, params.right_alias, params.right_field) });
+  }
+  if (stepType === 'union_sources') return renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.union_sources.default', { left_source: quotePhraseValue(params.union_left_source || ''), right_source: quotePhraseValue(params.union_right_source || ''), output_source: quotePhraseValue(params.output_source || step.source || '') });
+  if (stepType === 'format_field') return renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.format_field.default', { table_column: tableColumnPlain(params.source_file, params.source_alias, params.field_name), format: params.format || '', format_suffix: '' });
+  if (stepType === 'split_field') return renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.split_field.default', { table_column: tableColumnPlain(params.source_file, params.source_alias, params.field_name), split_on: quotePhraseValue(params.split_on || ''), split_part: params.split_part || '', output_column: quotePhraseValue(params.output_column || '') });
+  if (stepType === 'sort_records') return renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.sort_records.default', { table_column: tableColumnPlain(params.source_file, params.source_alias, params.field_name), sort_direction: params.sort_direction || 'Ascending' });
+  if (stepType === 'remove_duplicates') return renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.remove_duplicates.default', { table_column: tableColumnPlain(params.source_file, params.source_alias, params.field_name), duplicate_action: params.duplicate_action || 'Keep first record' });
+  if (stepType === 'filter_rows') return renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.filter_rows.default', { table_column: tableColumnPlain(params.source_file, params.source_alias, params.field_name), operator: params.operator || 'is equal to', compare_value: params.compare_value ? ` ${formattedValueList(params.compare_value)}` : '', filter_action: params.filter_action || 'Keep matching rows' });
+  if (stepType === 'flag_records') return `${renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.flag_records.condition', { table_column: tableColumnPlain(params.source_file, params.source_alias, params.field_name), operator: params.operator || 'is equal to', compare_value: params.compare_value ? ` ${formattedValueList(params.compare_value)}` : '' })} ${params.flag_action === 'do_not_display' ? renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.flag_records.do_not_display', {}) : renderCatalogPhrase('analyst_logic.flag_records.display_text', { message: quotePhraseValue(params.message || ''), output_column: quotePhraseValue(params.output_column || 'Error Message') })}`;
+  return `[Missing phrase path: analyst_logic.${stepType || 'unknown'}]`;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Generates notes for a step when manual notes are not provided.
+ * @param {any} step - Logic step object being read or modified.
+ * @param {any} index - Zero-based index used for ordering or display.
+ */
+function noteText(step, index) {
+  normalizeCatalogStep(step);
+  const params = step.params || {};
+  if (step.notes) return step.notes;
+  if (step.step_type === 'join') {
+    if (phraseIsUnionJoinType(params.join_type)) return renderCatalogPhrase('notes.union', { left_source: params.left_source || params.union_left_source || '[file 1]', right_source: params.right_source || params.union_right_source || '[file 2]', output_source: params.output_source || '[combined file]' });
+    return renderCatalogPhrase(`notes.join.${params.join_type || 'DEFAULT'}`, { left_alias: params.left_alias || params.left_source || '[file 1]', right_alias: params.right_alias || params.right_source || '[file 2]', keys: `${quotePhraseValue(params.left_field || '[file 1 key]')} / ${quotePhraseValue(params.right_field || '[file 2 key]')}` });
+  }
+  if (step.step_type === 'union_sources') return renderCatalogPhrase('notes.union', { left_source: params.union_left_source || '[file 1]', right_source: params.union_right_source || '[file 2]', output_source: params.output_source || '[combined file]' });
+  if (step.step_type === 'else') return renderCatalogPhrase('notes.else.default', {});
+  return ``;
+}

-// 14 provides the catalog-driven fallback renderer. 15-visual-ui-upgrades.js
-// replaces renderSteps with the final visual renderer after this file loads.
-renderSteps = renderStepsAuditWorkspace;
+/**
+ * 14-catalog-driven-builder.js
+ *
+ * Catalog-driven step normalization and base guided-builder rendering for each supported Logic Builder step type.
+ * Auto-generated documentation comments explain function purpose, expected inputs, and module sections without changing runtime behavior.
+ */
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Step type catalog
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+const STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS = [
+  ['reference_source', 'Reference Source'],
+  ['create_column', 'Create Column'],
+  ['if_then', 'IF / THEN'],
+  ['else', 'ELSE'],
+  ['join', 'Join / Union Sources'],
+  ['format_field', 'Format Field'],
+  ['split_field', 'Split Field'],
+  ['sort_records', 'Sort Records'],
+  ['remove_duplicates', 'Remove Duplicates'],
+  ['filter_rows', 'Filter Rows'],
+  ['flag_records', 'Flag Records'],
+];
+const LEGACY_TYPE_MAP = { source: 'reference_source', create: 'create_column', if: 'if_then', compare: 'if_then', join: 'join', transform: 'filter_rows' };
+/**
+ * Returns true when a Join Type value is Union.
+ * @param {any} joinType - Input used by isUnionJoinType.
+ */
+function isUnionJoinType(joinType) { return String(joinType || '').trim().toUpperCase() === 'UNION'; }
+/**
+ * Maps catalog step types back to legacy step type names.
+ * @param {any} stepType - Input used by legacyTypeFromStepType.
+ */
+function legacyTypeFromStepType(stepType) {
+  if (stepType === 'reference_source') return 'source';
+  if (stepType === 'create_column') return 'create';
+  if (stepType === 'join') return 'join';
+  if (stepType === 'if_then' || stepType === 'else' || stepType === 'filter_rows' || stepType === 'flag_records') return 'if';
+  return 'transform';
+}
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Step normalization
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/**
+ * Normalizes a step into the catalog-driven schema.
+ * @param {any} step - Logic step object being read or modified.
+ * @param {any} index - Zero-based index used for ordering or display.
+ */
+function normalizeCatalogStep(step, index = 0) {
+  if (!step) return step;
+  step.step_type = step.step_type || LEGACY_TYPE_MAP[step.type] || step.type || 'if_then';
+  step.type = legacyTypeFromStepType(step.step_type);
+  step.params = step.params && typeof step.params === 'object' ? step.params : {};
+  if (step.step_type === 'union_sources') {
+    step.step_type = 'join';
+    step.type = 'join';
+    step.params.join_type = 'UNION';
+    step.params.left_source = step.params.left_source || step.params.union_left_source || '';
+    step.params.right_source = step.params.right_source || step.params.union_right_source || '';
+    step.params.output_source = step.params.output_source || step.source || '';
+    step.params.left_alias = step.params.left_alias || sourceAliasFor(step.params.left_source);
+    step.params.right_alias = step.params.right_alias || sourceAliasFor(step.params.right_source);
+    step.params.left_field = '';
+    step.params.right_field = '';
+  }
+  if (step.source && !step.params.source_file) step.params.source_file = step.source;
+  if (step.field && !step.params.field_name && step.step_type !== 'create_column') step.params.field_name = step.field;
+  if (step.step_type === 'reference_source') {
+    step.params.source_file = step.params.source_file || step.source || state.audit.sources[0]?.name || '';
+    step.params.source_alias = step.params.source_alias || sourceAliasFor(step.params.source_file);
+  }
+  if (step.step_type === 'create_column') {
+    step.params.column_name = step.params.column_name || step.field || state.audit.fields[0]?.name || '';
+    step.params.value_mode = step.params.value_mode || 'Static value';
+    step.params.expression_type = step.params.expression_type || 'custom_expression';
+    // Legacy concatenate steps used one shared source. Assign an explicit source to each operand.
+    if (step.params.value_mode === 'Concatenate fields') {
+      step.params.concat_source_1 = step.params.concat_source_1 || step.params.source_file || step.source || '';
+      step.params.concat_source_2 = step.params.concat_source_2 || step.params.source_file || step.source || '';
+    }
+  }
+  if (step.step_type === 'if_then') {
+    const conditions = parseStepConditions(step);
+    if (!step.params.conditions_json) atlasWriteLiveEditorConditions(step, conditions.length ? conditions : [defaultGuidedCondition(step)]);
+    step.params.then_action_type = step.params.then_action_type || 'freeform';
+  }
+  if (step.step_type === 'join') step.params.join_type = step.params.join_type || 'INNER JOIN';
+  if (step.step_type === 'format_field') step.params.format = step.params.format || catalogArray('app_config.logic_options.format_options', ['text'])[0];
+  if (step.step_type === 'sort_records') step.params.sort_direction = step.params.sort_direction || 'Ascending';
+  if (step.step_type === 'remove_duplicates') step.params.duplicate_action = step.params.duplicate_action || 'Keep first record';
+  step.id = step.id || uid('step');
+  step.sortOrder = Number.isFinite(Number(step.sortOrder)) ? step.sortOrder : index + 1;
+  if (!step.title) step.title = STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS.find(([value]) => value === step.step_type)?.[1] || 'Logic Step';
+  return step;
+}
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Base guided-builder helpers
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/**
+ * Creates the default guided IF condition for a step.
+ * @param {any} step - Logic step object being read or modified.
+ */
+function defaultGuidedCondition(step) {
+  const sourceFile = step.params?.source_file || step.source || state.audit.sources[0]?.name || '';
+  return { id: uid('cond'), connector: 'IF', conditionType: 'row_compare', sourceFile, sourceAlias: sourceAliasFor(sourceFile), fieldName: step.params?.field_name || step.field || '', operator: 'is equal to', valueMode: 'values', compareValue: '' };
+}
+/**
+ * Builds option markup from strings or value/label pairs.
+ * @param {any} options - Configuration/options object.
+ * @param {any} selected - Currently selected value.
+ */
+function optionsHtml(options, selected) { return options.map((option) => Array.isArray(option) ? `<option value="${esc(option[0])}" ${selected === option[0] ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(option[1])}</option>` : `<option value="${esc(option)}" ${selected === option ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(option)}</option>`).join(''); }
+/**
+ * Renders a field picker input for a parameter.
+ * @param {any} name - Name, filename, or key used by the operation.
+ * @param {any} value - Value to process or assign.
+ * @param {any} placeholder - Input used by fieldInput.
+ * @param {any} sourceFile - Raw source file name.
+ */
+function fieldInput(name, value, placeholder = 'Select field', sourceFile = '') { return `<select data-param="${esc(name)}">${fieldOptionsHtml(sourceFile, value || '')}</select>`; }
+/**
+ * Renders a source picker input for a parameter.
+ * @param {any} name - Name, filename, or key used by the operation.
+ * @param {any} value - Value to process or assign.
+ * @param {any} placeholder - Input used by sourceInput.
+ */
+function sourceInput(name, value, placeholder = 'Select source file') { return `<select data-param="${esc(name)}">${sourceOptionsHtml(value || '')}</select>`; }
+/**
+ * Renders hidden source alias input markup.
+ * @param {any} params - Step params object.
+ */
+function sourceAliasHidden(params) { return `<input type="hidden" data-param="source_alias" value="${esc(params.source_alias || sourceAliasFor(params.source_file || ''))}">`; }
+
+
+
+/**
+ * Normalizes newline-delimited comparison input into one value per line.
+ * @param {any} value - Raw comparison value list.
+ */
+function valuesToLines(value) {
+  return String(value || '').split(/\r?\n/).map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean).join('\n');
+}
+
+/**
+ * Writes guided IF conditions back to a step in the canonical params/summary shape.
+ * @param {any} step - Step to update.
+ * @param {Array<any>} conditions - Guided condition list.
+ */
+function atlasWriteLiveEditorConditions(step, conditions) {
+  step.params = step.params && typeof step.params === 'object' ? step.params : {};
+  step.params.conditions_json = JSON.stringify(conditions || []);
+  step.conditions = (conditions || []).map((condition) => ({
+    id: condition.id,
+    field: condition.fieldName,
+    operator: condition.operator,
+    value: condition.compareValue,
+  }));
+}
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Catalog-driven editor rendering
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/**
+ * Renders the catalog-driven editor for a step type.
+ * @param {any} step - Logic step object being read or modified.
+ */
+function renderGuidedBuilder(step) {
+  normalizeCatalogStep(step);
+  const p = step.params || {};
+  if (step.step_type === 'reference_source') return `<div class="guided-builder"><h4>Reference Source</h4><label>Source File${sourceInput('source_file', p.source_file)}</label><label>Source Alias<input data-param="source_alias" value="${esc(p.source_alias || sourceAliasFor(p.source_file || ''))}"></label></div>`;
+  if (step.step_type === 'create_column') return `<div class="guided-builder"><h4>Create Column</h4><label>New Column Name<input data-param="column_name" value="${esc(p.column_name || '')}" placeholder="e.g., Error Message"></label><label>Column Value Type<select data-param="value_mode">${optionsHtml(catalogArray('app_config.logic_options.create_value_modes', ['Static value','Source field value','Expression / calculation','Concatenate fields']), p.value_mode)}</select></label>${renderCreateModeInputs(p)}</div>`;
+  if (step.step_type === 'if_then') return `<div class="guided-builder"><h4>IF Condition Builder</h4><div class="condition-guided-stack">${parseStepConditions(step).map((condition, index) => renderConditionRow(condition, index)).join('')}</div><div class="row"><button type="button" class="secondary small" data-guided-add-condition="AND">+ AND</button><button type="button" class="secondary small" data-guided-add-condition="OR">+ OR</button></div>${renderThenActionBuilder(p)}</div>`;
+  if (step.step_type === 'else') return `<div class="guided-builder"><h4>ELSE Action</h4><p class="hint">ELSE can apply an action or end with a colon so nested sub-steps can continue beneath it.</p>${renderThenActionBuilder(p, 'ELSE')}</div>`;
+  
+  if (step.step_type === 'join') {
+    const union = isUnionJoinType(p.join_type);
+    const joinOptions = ['INNER JOIN','LEFT JOIN','RIGHT JOIN','FULL OUTER JOIN','UNION'];
+    return `
+    <div class="guided-builder">
+      <h4>${union ? 'Union Sources' : 'Join / Union Sources'}</h4>
+      <label>
+        Join Type
+        <select data-param="join_type">
+          ${optionsHtml(joinOptions, p.join_type || 'INNER JOIN')}
+        </select>
+      </label>
+      <label>
+        Left Source
+        ${sourceInput('left_source', p.left_source)}
+      </label>
+      ${union ? '' : `<label>
+        Left Field
+        ${fieldInput('left_field', p.left_field, 'Select field', p.left_source)}
+      </label>`}
+      <label>
+        Right Source
+        ${sourceInput('right_source', p.right_source)}
+      </label>
+      ${union ? '' : `<label>
+        Right Field
+        ${fieldInput('right_field', p.right_field, 'Select field', p.right_source)}
+      </label>`}
+      ${union ? `<label>Output Source${sourceInput('output_source', p.output_source)}</label><p class="hint join-builder-union-hint">Union appends rows from both files into one combined output. No matching key is needed.</p>` : ''}
+    </div>`;
+  }
+  if (step.step_type === 'format_field') return `<div class="guided-builder"><h4>Format Field</h4><label>Source File${sourceInput('source_file', p.source_file)}</label>${sourceAliasHidden(p)}<label>Field${fieldInput('field_name', p.field_name, 'Select field', p.source_file)}</label><label>Format<select data-param="format">${optionsHtml(catalogArray('app_config.logic_options.format_options', ['text','number']), p.format)}</select></label></div>`;
+  if (step.step_type === 'split_field') return `<div class="guided-builder"><h4>Split Field</h4><label>Source File${sourceInput('source_file', p.source_file)}</label>${sourceAliasHidden(p)}<label>Field${fieldInput('field_name', p.field_name, 'Select field', p.source_file)}</label><label>Split On<input data-param="split_on" value="${esc(p.split_on || '')}"></label><label>Display Part<input data-param="split_part" value="${esc(p.split_part || '')}" placeholder="e.g., first value"></label><label>Output Column${fieldInput('output_column', p.output_column)}</label></div>`;
+  if (step.step_type === 'sort_records') return `<div class="guided-builder"><h4>Sort Records</h4><label>Source File${sourceInput('source_file', p.source_file)}</label>${sourceAliasHidden(p)}<label>Sort Field${fieldInput('field_name', p.field_name, 'Select field', p.source_file)}</label><label>Direction<select data-param="sort_direction">${optionsHtml(catalogArray('app_config.logic_options.sort_directions', ['Ascending','Descending']), p.sort_direction)}</select></label></div>`;
+  if (step.step_type === 'remove_duplicates') return `<div class="guided-builder"><h4>Remove Duplicates</h4><label>Source File${sourceInput('source_file', p.source_file)}</label>${sourceAliasHidden(p)}<label>Field${fieldInput('field_name', p.field_name, 'Select field', p.source_file)}</label><label>Duplicate Action<select data-param="duplicate_action">${optionsHtml(catalogArray('app_config.logic_options.duplicate_actions', ['Keep first record','Keep last record','Drop all duplicated records']), p.duplicate_action)}</select></label></div>`;
+  if (step.step_type === 'filter_rows' || step.step_type === 'flag_records') return `<div class="guided-builder"><h4>${step.step_type === 'filter_rows' ? 'Filter Rows' : 'Flag Records'}</h4><label>Source File${sourceInput('source_file', p.source_file)}</label>${sourceAliasHidden(p)}<label>Field${fieldInput('field_name', p.field_name, 'Select field', p.source_file)}</label><label>Operator<select data-param="operator">${optionsHtml(catalogArray('app_config.logic_options.operators', ['is equal to']), p.operator)}</select></label><label>Compare Value<textarea data-param="compare_value" placeholder="One or more values">${esc(p.compare_value || '')}</textarea></label>${step.step_type === 'filter_rows' ? `<label>Filter Action<select data-param="filter_action">${optionsHtml(catalogArray('app_config.logic_options.filter_actions', ['Keep matching rows','Exclude matching rows']), p.filter_action)}</select></label>` : `<label>Flag Action<select data-param="flag_action"><option value="display_text" ${p.flag_action !== 'do_not_display' ? 'selected' : ''}>Display text response</option><option value="do_not_display" ${p.flag_action === 'do_not_display' ? 'selected' : ''}>Do not display record</option></select></label><label>Message<input data-param="message" value="${esc(p.message || '')}"></label><label>Output Column${fieldInput('output_column', p.output_column || 'Error Message')}</label>`}</div>`;
+  return '';
+}
+/**
+ * Renders Create Column inputs for the selected value mode.
+ * @param {any} p - Input used by renderCreateModeInputs.
+ */
+function renderCreateModeInputs(p) {
+  if (p.value_mode === 'Static value') return `<label>Static Value<input data-param="static_value" value="${esc(p.static_value || '')}"></label>`;
+  if (p.value_mode === 'Source field value') return `<label>Source File${sourceInput('source_file', p.source_file)}</label>${sourceAliasHidden(p)}<label>Source Field${fieldInput('field_name', p.field_name, 'Select field', p.source_file)}</label>`;
+  if (p.value_mode === 'Concatenate fields') return `<label>First Source${sourceInput('concat_source_1', p.concat_source_1 || p.source_file)}</label><label>First Field${fieldInput('concat_field_1', p.concat_field_1, 'Select field', p.concat_source_1 || p.source_file)}</label><label>Second Source${sourceInput('concat_source_2', p.concat_source_2 || p.source_file)}</label><label>Second Field${fieldInput('concat_field_2', p.concat_field_2, 'Select field', p.concat_source_2 || p.source_file)}</label>`;
+  return `<label>Expression Type<select data-param="expression_type"><option value="custom_expression" ${p.expression_type === 'custom_expression' ? 'selected' : ''}>Custom Expression</option><option value="first_characters" ${p.expression_type === 'first_characters' ? 'selected' : ''}>First Characters</option><option value="character_count" ${p.expression_type === 'character_count' ? 'selected' : ''}>Character Count</option></select></label><label>Expression / Calculation<textarea data-param="expression">${esc(p.expression || '')}</textarea></label><label>Source File${sourceInput('source_file', p.source_file)}</label>${sourceAliasHidden(p)}<label>Expression Source Field${fieldInput('expression_source_field', p.expression_source_field, 'Select field', p.source_file)}</label><label>Number of Characters<input data-param="character_count" value="${esc(p.character_count || '')}"></label>`;
+}
+function compareMathOperationsForCondition(condition = {}) {
+  return typeof parseSourceMathOperations === 'function'
+    ? parseSourceMathOperations(condition.compareMathJson || condition.compare_math_json || condition.compareMath || [])
+    : [];
+}
+
+function compareMathOperatorOptions(selected = '*') {
+  return (typeof ATLAS_SOURCE_MATH_OPERATORS !== 'undefined' ? ATLAS_SOURCE_MATH_OPERATORS : ['+', '-', '*', '/'])
+    .map((operator) => `<option value="${esc(operator)}" ${operator === selected ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(operator)}</option>`).join('');
+}
+
+function renderCompareMathOperationRow(operation = {}, fallbackSourceFile = '') {
+  const id = operation.id || uid('math');
+  const operator = (typeof ATLAS_SOURCE_MATH_OPERATORS !== 'undefined' && ATLAS_SOURCE_MATH_OPERATORS.includes(operation.operator)) ? operation.operator : '*';
+  const operandType = operation.operandType === 'field' ? 'field' : 'number';
+  const operandSource = operation.operandSourceFile || fallbackSourceFile;
+  return `<div class="compare-math-operation-row condition-value-row" data-compare-math-row data-compare-math-id="${esc(id)}">
+    <select data-compare-math-operator aria-label="Math operator">${compareMathOperatorOptions(operator)}</select>
+    <select data-compare-math-operand-type aria-label="Operand type"><option value="number" ${operandType === 'number' ? 'selected' : ''}>Number</option><option value="field" ${operandType === 'field' ? 'selected' : ''}>Field</option></select>
+    <input data-compare-math-operand value="${esc(operation.operand || '')}" placeholder="Value" inputmode="decimal" ${operandType === 'field' ? 'hidden' : ''}>
+    <select data-compare-math-operand-source ${operandType === 'field' ? '' : 'hidden'}>${sourceOptionsHtml(operandSource)}</select>
+    <select data-compare-math-operand-field ${operandType === 'field' ? '' : 'hidden'}>${fieldOptionsHtml(operandSource, operation.operandFieldName || '')}</select>
+    <button type="button" class="secondary small" data-remove-compare-math-operation>Remove</button>
+  </div>`;
+}
+
+function renderCompareMathEditor(condition = {}, compareSourceFile = '') {
+  const operations = compareMathOperationsForCondition(condition);
+  const hiddenValue = JSON.stringify(operations);
+  return `<div class="compare-math-editor condition-values-editor" data-compare-math-editor>
+    <div class="condition-values-header"><label>Compare Field Math<input type="hidden" data-condition-field="compareMathJson" value="${esc(hiddenValue)}"></label><button type="button" class="secondary small" data-add-compare-math-operation>Add Operation</button></div>
+    <p class="hint full">Optional operations are applied to the compare field in order.</p>
+    <div class="compare-math-operations-list condition-values-list" data-compare-math-list>${operations.map((operation) => renderCompareMathOperationRow(operation, compareSourceFile)).join('')}</div>
+  </div>`;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Renders one guided IF/THEN condition row.
+ * @param {any} condition - Condition object to format or render.
+ * @param {any} index - Zero-based index used for ordering or display.
+ */
+function renderConditionRow(condition, index) {
+  const sourceFile = condition.sourceFile || '';
+  const valueMode = condition.valueMode || 'values';
+  const compareSource = condition.compareSourceFile || sourceFile;
+  return `<div class="guided-condition-row" data-condition-index="${index}" data-condition-id="${esc(condition.id || uid('cond'))}">
+    <select data-condition-field="connector"><option ${index === 0 ? 'selected' : ''}>IF</option><option ${condition.connector === 'AND' ? 'selected' : ''}>AND</option><option ${condition.connector === 'OR' ? 'selected' : ''}>OR</option></select>
+    <label>Source File<select data-condition-field="sourceFile">${sourceOptionsHtml(sourceFile)}</select></label>
+    <label>Field<select data-condition-field="fieldName">${fieldOptionsHtml(sourceFile, condition.fieldName || '')}</select></label>
+    <label>Operator<select data-condition-field="operator">${optionsHtml(catalogArray('app_config.logic_options.operators', ['is equal to']), condition.operator)}</select></label>
+    <label>Value Mode<select data-condition-field="valueMode"><option value="values" ${valueMode === 'values' ? 'selected' : ''}>Values</option><option value="field" ${valueMode === 'field' ? 'selected' : ''}>Compare to Field</option><option value="blank" ${valueMode === 'blank' ? 'selected' : ''}>Blank / Null</option></select></label>
+    ${valueMode === 'field'
+      ? `<label>Compare Source<select data-condition-field="compareSourceFile">${sourceOptionsHtml(compareSource)}</select></label><label>Compare Field<select data-condition-field="compareFieldName">${fieldOptionsHtml(compareSource, condition.compareFieldName || '')}</select></label>${renderCompareMathEditor(condition, compareSource)}`
+      : valueMode === 'blank'
+        ? `<div class="logic-editor-callout">No comparison value required.</div>`
+        : `<label>Values<textarea data-condition-field="compareValue" placeholder="One value per line">${esc(condition.compareValue || condition.expression || '')}</textarea></label>`}
+    <button type="button" class="danger small" data-guided-delete-condition>Delete</button>
+  </div>`;
+}
+
+/**
+ * Renders THEN/ELSE action controls.
+ * @param {any} p - Input used by renderThenActionBuilder.
+ * @param {any} prefix - Action label prefix such as THEN or ELSE.
+ */
+function renderThenActionBuilder(p, prefix = 'THEN') {
+  return `<div class="then-action-builder"><h4>${prefix} Action</h4><label>${prefix} Action Type<select data-param="then_action_type"><option value="freeform" ${p.then_action_type === 'freeform' ? 'selected' : ''}>Freeform Text</option><option value="set_column_value" ${p.then_action_type === 'set_column_value' ? 'selected' : ''}>Set Column Value from Field</option><option value="display_text" ${p.then_action_type === 'display_text' ? 'selected' : ''}>Display Text Response in Column</option><option value="do_not_display" ${p.then_action_type === 'do_not_display' ? 'selected' : ''}>Do Not Display</option></select></label><label>${prefix} Action<textarea data-param="then_action" placeholder="e.g., display records in the audit">${esc(p.then_action || '')}</textarea></label><label>Display Text<input data-param="then_text" value="${esc(p.then_text || '')}" placeholder="e.g., Research IT0027"></label><label>Output Column${fieldInput('then_output_column', p.then_output_column || 'Error Message')}</label><label>Target Column${fieldInput('then_target_column', p.then_target_column)}</label><label>Value Source${sourceInput('then_value_source_file', p.then_value_source_file || p.source_file)}</label><label>Value Field${fieldInput('then_value_field', p.then_value_field, 'Select field', p.then_value_source_file || p.source_file)}</label></div>`;
+}
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Base catalog-driven rules rendering
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/**
+ * Handles the renderSteps portion of the ATLAS Align client-side workflow.
+ */
+function renderStepsCatalogDrivenBase() {
+  state.audit.steps.forEach((step, index) => normalizeCatalogStep(step, index));
+  const entries = hierarchicalStepEntries({ includeDisabled: true });
+  e.stepsList.innerHTML = entries.map((entry) => {
+    const step = entry.step;
+    return `<div class="card step-card ${state.selectedStepId === step.id ? 'selected' : ''} ${step.parentId ? `branch-${step.branchType || 'then'}` : 'branch-root'}" data-id="${esc(step.id)}" data-parent-id="${esc(step.parentId || '')}" style="--depth:${entry.level}"><div class="step-header"><span class="step-title"><span class="step-number">${esc(entry.number)}</span>${esc(STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS.find(([value]) => value === step.step_type)?.[1] || step.step_type)}</span><span class="badge branch-badge">${esc(entry.branchLabel)}</span><span class="badge">${esc(STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS.find(([value]) => value === step.step_type)?.[1] || step.step_type)}</span></div><div class="hierarchy-controls"><label>Parent Step<select data-step-parent>${parentOptionsForStep(step).map((option) => `<option value="${esc(option.id)}" ${(step.parentId || '') === option.id ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(option.label)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>Branch Type<select data-step-branch>${branchTypeOptions(step)}</select></label></div><input type="hidden" data-step-title value="${esc(step.title || STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS.find(([value]) => value === step.step_type)?.[1] || 'Logic Step')}"><label>SWAT Step Type<select data-step-type>${optionsHtml(STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS, step.step_type)}</select></label><input type="hidden" data-step-source value="${esc(step.source || '')}"><input type="hidden" data-step-field value="${esc(step.field || '')}"><input type="hidden" data-step-action value="${esc(step.action || '')}">${renderGuidedBuilder(step)}<label>Manual Notes<textarea data-step-notes>${esc(step.notes || '')}</textarea></label><label><span class="check"><input type="checkbox" data-step-enabled ${step.enabled !== false ? 'checked' : ''}> Enabled</span></label><div class="logic-preview-small"><strong>Generated Analyst Logic</strong><p>${esc(analystLogic(step))}</p></div><div class="row hierarchy-buttons"><button class="secondary small" data-select-step>Select</button><button class="small" data-add-child-step>Add Child Step</button><button class="secondary small" data-indent>Indent</button><button class="secondary small" data-outdent>Outdent</button><button class="danger small" data-delete-step>Delete Step</button></div></div>`;
+  }).join('');
+  renderSelectedStep();
+}
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Catalog-driven capture override
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+const baseCapture = capture;
+/**
+ * Reads current DOM controls into the in-memory audit state.
+ */
+capture = function captureCatalogDriven() {
+  baseCapture();
+  document.querySelectorAll('.step-card').forEach((card) => {
+    const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === card.dataset.id);
+    if (!step) return;
+    step.step_type = card.querySelector('[data-step-type]')?.value || step.step_type || 'if_then';
+    step.type = legacyTypeFromStepType(step.step_type);
+    step.params = step.params && typeof step.params === 'object' ? step.params : {};
+    card.querySelectorAll('[data-param]').forEach((input) => {
+      if (input.type === 'checkbox') step.params[input.dataset.param] = input.checked ? 'Yes' : 'No';
+      else step.params[input.dataset.param] = input.value;
+    });
+    const conditionRows = [...card.querySelectorAll('.guided-condition-row')];
+    if (conditionRows.length) {
+      const conditions = conditionRows.map((row, index) => {
+        /**
+         * Handles the get portion of the ATLAS Align client-side workflow.
+         * @param {any} field - Field record.
+         */
+        const get = (field) => row.querySelector(`[data-condition-field="${field}"]`)?.value || '';
+        const sourceFile = get('sourceFile');
+        const compareSourceFile = get('compareSourceFile') || sourceFile;
+        return { id: row.dataset.conditionId || uid('cond'), connector: index === 0 ? 'IF' : get('connector'), conditionType: 'row_compare', sourceFile, sourceAlias: sourceAliasFor(sourceFile), fieldName: get('fieldName'), operator: get('operator'), valueMode: get('valueMode') || 'values', compareValue: get('compareValue'), compareFieldName: get('compareFieldName'), compareSourceFile, compareSourceAlias: sourceAliasFor(compareSourceFile), expression: get('expression'), compareMathJson: get('compareMathJson') };
+      });
+      atlasWriteLiveEditorConditions(step, conditions);
+    }
+    if (step.params.source_file) { step.source = step.params.source_file; step.params.source_alias = sourceAliasFor(step.params.source_file); }
+    if (step.params.field_name) step.field = step.params.field_name;
+    if (step.params.column_name) step.field = step.params.column_name;
+  });
+};
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// ELSE branch actions
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+/**
+ * Creates or updates an ELSE child step for an IF/THEN step.
+ * @param {any} ifStep - Input used by createOrUpdateElseStep.
+ */
+function atlasConditionSummaries(conditions) {
+  return (conditions || []).map((condition) => ({
+    id: condition.id,
+    field: condition.fieldName || condition.field || '',
+    operator: condition.operator || 'is equal to',
+    value: condition.compareValue || condition.value || condition.expression || '',
+  }));
+}
+
+function atlasCloneInverseConditionsForTarget(sourceStep, targetStep = null) {
+  const inverted = typeof invertedConditionsForStep === 'function' ? invertedConditionsForStep(sourceStep) : [];
+  const existing = targetStep ? parseStepConditions(targetStep) : [];
+  return inverted.map((condition, index) => ({
+    ...condition,
+    id: existing[index]?.id || condition.id || uid('cond'),
+  }));
+}
+
+function atlasSourceForInverseStep(sourceStep, conditions) {
+  return sourceStep?.params?.source_file || sourceStep?.source || conditions?.[0]?.sourceFile || '';
+}
+
+function atlasInverseElseSortOrder(sourceStep) {
+  const siblings = childrenOf(sourceStep.parentId || null);
+  const index = siblings.findIndex((step) => step.id === sourceStep.id);
+  const current = Number(sourceStep.sortOrder) || index + 1;
+  const next = siblings[index + 1];
+  if (next) return (current + (Number(next.sortOrder) || index + 2)) / 2;
+  return current + 1;
+}
+
+function syncInverseElseStepsForSource(ifStepId) {
+  const sourceStep = state.audit.steps.find((step) => step.id === ifStepId && step.step_type === 'if_then');
+  if (!sourceStep) return false;
+  let changed = false;
+  (state.audit.steps || []).forEach((step) => {
+    if (step?.params?.inverse_else !== 'Yes' || step.params.inverse_of_step_id !== ifStepId) return;
+    const conditions = atlasCloneInverseConditionsForTarget(sourceStep, step);
+    const sourceFile = atlasSourceForInverseStep(sourceStep, conditions);
+    const sourceAlias = sourceAliasFor(sourceFile);
+    step.params = step.params && typeof step.params === 'object' ? step.params : {};
+    atlasWriteLiveEditorConditions(step, conditions);
+    step.params.source_file = sourceFile;
+    step.params.source_alias = sourceAlias;
+    step.source = sourceFile;
+    changed = true;
+  });
+  return changed;
+}
+
+function createInverseElseStepForIf(stepId) {
+  capture?.();
+  const sourceStep = state.audit.steps.find((step) => step.id === stepId);
+  if (!sourceStep || sourceStep.autoGeneratedReference || sourceStep.step_type !== 'if_then') {
+    status?.('Create inverse step (ELSE) is only available for manual IF steps.', 'warn');
+    return null;
+  }
+  normalizeCatalogStep(sourceStep);
+  const conditions = atlasCloneInverseConditionsForTarget(sourceStep);
+  const sourceFile = atlasSourceForInverseStep(sourceStep, conditions);
+  const sourceAlias = sourceAliasFor(sourceFile);
+  const inverseStep = {
+    id: uid('step'),
+    enabled: true,
+    parentId: sourceStep.parentId || null,
+    branchType: sourceStep.branchType || (sourceStep.parentId ? 'then' : 'root'),
+    sortOrder: atlasInverseElseSortOrder(sourceStep),
+    title: 'Inverse ELSE',
+    type: 'if',
+    step_type: 'if_then',
+    source: sourceFile,
+    field: '',
+    action: '',
+    notes: '',
+    conditions: atlasConditionSummaries(conditions),
+    params: {
+      inverse_else: 'Yes',
+      inverse_of_step_id: sourceStep.id,
+      source_file: sourceFile,
+      source_alias: sourceAlias,
+      conditions_json: JSON.stringify(conditions),
+      then_action_type: 'freeform',
+      then_action: '',
+    },
+  };
+  const entries = hierarchicalStepEntries({ includeDisabled: true });
+  const sourceEntryIndex = entries.findIndex((entry) => entry.step.id === sourceStep.id);
+  let afterStep = sourceStep;
+  for (let index = sourceEntryIndex + 1; index >= 0 && index < entries.length; index += 1) {
+    if (isDescendantOf(sourceStep.id, entries[index].step.id)) afterStep = entries[index].step;
+    else break;
+  }
+  const steps = state.audit.steps || [];
+  const insertIndex = Math.max(steps.findIndex((step) => step.id === afterStep.id), steps.findIndex((step) => step.id === sourceStep.id));
+  if (insertIndex >= 0) steps.splice(insertIndex + 1, 0, inverseStep);
+  else steps.push(inverseStep);
+  state.audit.steps = steps;
+  syncInverseElseStepsForSource(sourceStep.id);
+  atlasNormalizeStepOrderGroups?.();
+  state.selectedStepId = inverseStep.id;
+  renderAll();
+  showSection?.('steps');
+  scheduleAutosave();
+  status?.('Created inverse ELSE step.', 'ok');
+  return inverseStep;
+}
+
+function createOrUpdateElseStep(ifStep) {
+  // Legacy helper kept for existing references. New ELSE behavior uses linked inverse IF steps.
+  return createInverseElseStepForIf(ifStep?.id || ifStep);
+}
+document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
+  const card = event.target.closest('.step-card');
+  if (!card) return;
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === card.dataset.id);
+  if (!step) return;
+  if (event.target.matches('[data-guided-add-condition]')) {
+    capture();
+    const conditions = parseStepConditions(step);
+    conditions.push({ ...defaultGuidedCondition(step), connector: event.target.dataset.guidedAddCondition });
+    atlasWriteLiveEditorConditions(step, conditions);
+    syncInverseElseStepsForSource?.(step.id);
+    renderAll();
+    scheduleAutosave();
+  }
+  if (event.target.matches('[data-guided-delete-condition]')) {
+    capture();
+    const index = Number(event.target.closest('.guided-condition-row')?.dataset.conditionIndex || 0);
+    const conditions = parseStepConditions(step).filter((_condition, conditionIndex) => conditionIndex !== index);
+    atlasWriteLiveEditorConditions(step, conditions.length ? conditions : [defaultGuidedCondition(step)]);
+    syncInverseElseStepsForSource?.(step.id);
+    renderAll();
+    scheduleAutosave();
+  }
+});
+
+
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Audit workspace render override
+// -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+// Overrides for the audit-workspace pane redesign: generated reference steps are locked and shown first.
+/**
+ * Handles the renderSteps portion of the ATLAS Align client-side workflow.
+ */
+function renderStepsAuditWorkspace() {
+  state.audit.steps.forEach((step, index) => normalizeCatalogStep(step, index));
+  const entries = hierarchicalStepEntries({ includeDisabled: true });
+  e.stepsList.innerHTML = entries.map((entry) => {
+    const step = entry.step;
+    if (step.autoGeneratedReference) {
+      return `<div class="card step-card generated-reference-step ${state.selectedStepId === step.id ? 'selected' : ''}" data-id="${esc(step.id)}" data-parent-id="" style="--depth:${entry.level}">
+        <div class="step-header"><span class="step-title"><span class="step-number">${esc(entry.number)}</span>Reference Source</span><span class="badge">Generated</span><span class="badge">Locked</span></div>
+        <p class="hint">This reference step is generated from the source files selected in Sources & Fields.</p>
+        <div class="logic-preview-small"><strong>Generated Analyst Logic</strong><p>${esc(analystLogic(step))}</p></div>
+        <div class="row hierarchy-buttons"><button class="secondary small" data-select-step>Select</button></div>
+      </div>`;
+    }
+    return `<div class="card step-card ${state.selectedStepId === step.id ? 'selected' : ''} ${step.parentId ? `branch-${step.branchType || 'then'}` : 'branch-root'}" data-id="${esc(step.id)}" data-parent-id="${esc(step.parentId || '')}" style="--depth:${entry.level}">
+      <div class="step-header"><span class="step-title"><span class="step-number">${esc(entry.number)}</span>${esc(STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS.find(([value]) => value === step.step_type)?.[1] || step.step_type)}</span><span class="badge branch-badge">${esc(entry.branchLabel)}</span><span class="badge">${esc(STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS.find(([value]) => value === step.step_type)?.[1] || step.step_type)}</span></div>
+      <div class="hierarchy-controls"><label>Parent Step<select data-step-parent>${parentOptionsForStep(step).map((option) => `<option value="${esc(option.id)}" ${(step.parentId || '') === option.id ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(option.label)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>Branch Type<select data-step-branch>${branchTypeOptions(step)}</select></label></div>
+      <input type="hidden" data-step-title value="${esc(step.title || STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS.find(([value]) => value === step.step_type)?.[1] || 'Logic Step')}">
+      <label>SWAT Step Type<select data-step-type>${optionsHtml(STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS, step.step_type)}</select></label>
+      <input type="hidden" data-step-source value="${esc(step.source || '')}"><input type="hidden" data-step-field value="${esc(step.field || '')}"><input type="hidden" data-step-action value="${esc(step.action || '')}">
+      ${renderGuidedBuilder(step)}
+      <label>Manual Notes<textarea data-step-notes>${esc(step.notes || '')}</textarea></label>
+      <label><span class="check"><input type="checkbox" data-step-enabled ${step.enabled !== false ? 'checked' : ''}> Enabled</span></label>
+      <div class="logic-preview-small"><strong>Generated Analyst Logic</strong><p>${esc(analystLogic(step))}</p></div>
+      <div class="row hierarchy-buttons"><button class="secondary small" data-select-step>Select</button><button class="small" data-add-child-step>Add Child Step</button><button class="secondary small" data-indent>Indent</button><button class="secondary small" data-outdent>Outdent</button><button class="danger small" data-delete-step>Delete Step</button></div>
+    </div>`;
+  }).join('');
+  renderSelectedStep();
+}

+// 14 provides the catalog-driven fallback renderer. 15-visual-ui-upgrades.js
+// replaces renderSteps with the final visual renderer after this file loads.
+renderSteps = renderStepsAuditWorkspace;
