+let activeSourceLibraryTab = 'all';
+let visualPickerState = null;
+const collapsedStepIds = new Set();
+let draggedLogicStepId = '';
+let dropTargetLogicStepId = '';
+
+function sourceSystem(source) {
+  const text = `${source.sourceFile || ''} ${source.alias || ''}`.toLowerCase();
+  if (text.includes('workday') || text.includes('wd.') || text.includes('wd ') || text.startsWith('wd')) return 'workday';
+  if (text.includes('sap') || text.includes('sap.') || /^zhr|^zhre|^hi\d|^pa\s/i.test(source.sourceFile || '')) return 'sap';
+  return 'other';
+}
+
+function auditLibraryGroups() {
+  const groups = [];
+  if (repoLibraryState?.audits?.length) groups.push({ key: 'repo', label: 'Repo Audits', items: repoLibraryState.audits.map((audit) => ({ value: `repo:${audit.id}`, title: audit.name, detail: audit.description || audit.file || '' })) });
+  if (state.savedFlows?.length) groups.push({ key: 'local', label: 'Local Saved Audits', items: state.savedFlows.map((flow) => ({ value: `local:${flow.id}`, title: flow.name, detail: flow.updatedAt ? new Date(flow.updatedAt).toLocaleString() : 'Saved in this browser' })) });
+  if (typeof workbookState !== 'undefined' && workbookState.loaded && workbookState.auditSheets?.length) groups.push({ key: 'workbook', label: 'Workbook Imports', items: workbookState.auditSheets.map((name) => ({ value: `workbook:${name}`, title: name, detail: 'Detected workbook audit sheet' })) });
+  return groups;
+}
+
+renderAuditLibrarySelect = function renderAuditLibrarySelectVisual() {
+  const select = $('auditLibrarySelect');
+  const visual = $('auditLibraryVisual');
+  const groups = auditLibraryGroups();
+  if (select) {
+    select.innerHTML = groups.length ? groups.map((group) => `<optgroup label="${esc(group.label)}">${group.items.map((item) => `<option value="${esc(item.value)}">${esc(item.title)}</option>`).join('')}</optgroup>`).join('') : '<option value="">No audits available</option>';
+  }
+  if (!visual) return;
+  if (!groups.length) {
+    visual.innerHTML = '<p class="hint">No repo or local audits are available yet.</p>';
+    return;
+  }
+  visual.innerHTML = groups.map((group) => `<section class="visual-picker-group audit-library-group"><div class="visual-picker-group-heading"><span class="visual-picker-group-title">${esc(group.label)}</span><span class="visual-picker-group-count">${group.items.length}</span></div><div class="visual-picker-pill-grid">${group.items.map((item) => `<button type="button" class="visual-picker-pill audit-library-pill" data-audit-library-value="${esc(item.value)}"><span class="visual-picker-pill-main">${esc(item.title)}</span>${item.detail ? `<span class="visual-picker-pill-detail">${esc(item.detail)}</span>` : ''}</button>`).join('')}</div></section>`).join('');
+};
+
+function selectedSourceSet() {
+  return new Set((state.audit.sources || []).map((source) => source.name));
+}
+
+function sourceMatchesSearch(source, query) {
+  if (!query) return true;
+  return [source.sourceFile, source.alias, ...(source.fields || []).flatMap((field) => [field.fieldName, field.vanityLabel, field.label])].join(' ').toLowerCase().includes(query);
+}
+
+renderSourceFieldBrowser = function renderSourceFieldBrowserVisual() {
+  const panel = $('sourceFieldBrowser');
+  const tabRoot = $('sourceLibraryTabs');
+  if (!panel) return;
+  const allSources = sourceCatalogEntries();
+  const selected = selectedSourceSet();
+  const query = ($('sourceFieldSearch')?.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
+  const tabs = [
+    ['all', 'All', allSources],
+    ['workday', 'Workday', allSources.filter((source) => sourceSystem(source) === 'workday')],
+    ['sap', 'SAP', allSources.filter((source) => sourceSystem(source) === 'sap')],
+    ['other', 'Other / Unknown', allSources.filter((source) => sourceSystem(source) === 'other')],
+    ['selected', 'Selected', allSources.filter((source) => selected.has(source.sourceFile))],
+  ];
+  if (tabRoot) {
+    tabRoot.innerHTML = tabs.map(([key, label, items]) => `<button type="button" class="source-library-tab ${activeSourceLibraryTab === key ? 'active' : ''}" data-source-tab="${key}">${esc(label)} <span>${items.length}</span></button>`).join('');
+  }
+  const activeSources = (tabs.find(([key]) => key === activeSourceLibraryTab)?.[2] || allSources).filter((source) => sourceMatchesSearch(source, query));
+  panel.innerHTML = activeSources.length ? activeSources.map((source) => {
+    const system = sourceSystem(source);
+    const isSelected = selected.has(source.sourceFile);
+    const fields = query
+      ? (source.fields || []).filter((field) => `${field.fieldName} ${field.vanityLabel || ''} ${field.label || ''}`.toLowerCase().includes(query)).slice(0, 180)
+      : (source.fields || []).slice(0, 48);
+    return `<section class="visual-picker-group source-library-card ${isSelected ? 'selected-library-source' : ''}" data-source-system="${system}">
+      <div class="visual-picker-group-heading source-library-card-heading">
+        <div><div class="visual-picker-group-title">${esc(source.sourceFile)}</div><div class="visual-picker-modal-subtitle">${esc(source.alias || source.sourceFile)} · ${system === 'sap' ? 'SAP' : system === 'workday' ? 'Workday' : 'Unknown source'}</div></div>
+        <div class="visual-picker-group-meta"><span class="visual-picker-group-count">${source.fields?.length || 0} fields</span>${isSelected ? '<span class="badge selected-badge">Selected</span>' : ''}</div>
+      </div>
+      <div class="source-library-actions"><button type="button" class="${isSelected ? 'secondary' : ''} small" data-add-source-to-audit="${esc(source.sourceFile)}">${isSelected ? 'Refresh Source Fields' : 'Use Source in Audit'}</button></div>
+      <div class="visual-picker-pill-grid source-field-pill-grid">
+        ${fields.map((field) => {
+          const raw = field.fieldName || field.name || field.label || '';
+          const label = field.vanityLabel || field.label || raw;
+          return `<button type="button" class="visual-picker-pill source-field-pill" data-source-file="${esc(source.sourceFile)}" data-field-name="${esc(raw)}"><span class="visual-picker-pill-main">${esc(label)}</span><span class="visual-picker-pill-detail">${esc(raw)}</span></button>`;
+        }).join('')}
+      </div>
+      ${(source.fields?.length || 0) > fields.length ? `<p class="hint">Showing ${fields.length} of ${source.fields.length} fields. Search to narrow the list.</p>` : ''}
+    </section>`;
+  }).join('') : '<p class="hint">No source files or fields match the current search.</p>';
+};
+
+function pickerSourceOptions() {
+  return sourceCatalogEntries().map((source) => ({ sourceFile: source.sourceFile, sourceAlias: source.alias || source.sourceFile, fieldName: '', label: source.sourceFile, detail: source.alias || source.sourceFile, sourceColor: (state.audit.sources || []).find((item) => item.name === source.sourceFile)?.color || '' }));
+}
+
+function pickerFieldOptions(sourceFile = '') {
+  const sources = sourceFile ? sourceCatalogEntries().filter((source) => source.sourceFile === sourceFile) : sourceCatalogEntries();
+  return sources.flatMap((source) => (source.fields || []).map((field) => {
+    const raw = field.fieldName || field.name || field.label || '';
+    const label = field.vanityLabel || field.label || raw;
+    return { sourceFile: source.sourceFile, sourceAlias: source.alias || source.sourceFile, fieldName: raw, label, detail: raw, sourceColor: (state.audit.sources || []).find((item) => item.name === source.sourceFile)?.color || '' };
+  }));
+}
+
+function renderMiniPickerTrigger(kind, name, value, label, sourceFile = '') {
+  const detail = kind === 'source'
+    ? (sourceAliasFor?.(value) || '')
+    : sourceFile ? `${sourceFile}.${value || '[field]'}` : value || '';
+  const color = kind === 'field' ? ((state.audit.sources || []).find((source) => source.name === sourceFile)?.color || '') : ((state.audit.sources || []).find((source) => source.name === value)?.color || '');
+  return `<div class="visual-picker-trigger-row mini-picker-trigger-row"><input type="hidden" data-param="${esc(name)}" value="${esc(value || '')}"><button type="button" class="visual-picker-trigger-button mini-picker-trigger" data-open-mini-picker="${kind}" data-param-name="${esc(name)}" data-picker-source="${esc(sourceFile || '')}"><span class="visual-picker-trigger-main">${color ? `<span class="source-color-dot" style="background:${esc(color)}"></span>` : ''}<strong>${esc(value || label || 'Choose...')}</strong>${detail ? `<span class="visual-picker-pill-detail">${esc(detail)}</span>` : ''}</span><span class="visual-picker-trigger-caret">⌄</span></button></div>`;
+}
+
+if (typeof sourceInput === 'function') {
+  sourceInput = function visualSourceInput(name, value, placeholder = 'Select source file') {
+    return renderMiniPickerTrigger('source', name, value, placeholder);
+  };
+}
+if (typeof fieldInput === 'function') {
+  fieldInput = function visualFieldInput(name, value, placeholder = 'Select field', sourceFile = '') {
+    return renderMiniPickerTrigger('field', name, value, placeholder, sourceFile);
+  };
+}
+
+if (typeof renderConditionRow === 'function') {
+  renderConditionRow = function visualConditionRow(condition, index) {
+    const sourceFile = condition.sourceFile || '';
+    const valueMode = condition.valueMode || 'values';
+    return `<div class="guided-condition-row visual-condition-card" data-condition-index="${index}" data-condition-id="${esc(condition.id || uid('cond'))}">
+      <div class="condition-connector-row"><button type="button" class="condition-connector ${index === 0 ? 'active' : ''}" data-condition-set="connector" data-condition-value="IF">IF</button><button type="button" class="condition-connector ${condition.connector === 'AND' ? 'active' : ''}" data-condition-set="connector" data-condition-value="AND">AND</button><button type="button" class="condition-connector ${condition.connector === 'OR' ? 'active' : ''}" data-condition-set="connector" data-condition-value="OR">OR</button><input type="hidden" data-condition-field="connector" value="${esc(index === 0 ? 'IF' : condition.connector || 'AND')}"></div>
+      <label>Source File${renderConditionPickerTrigger('source', 'sourceFile', sourceFile, sourceFile || 'Choose source')}</label>
+      <label>Field${renderConditionPickerTrigger('field', 'fieldName', condition.fieldName || '', condition.fieldName || 'Choose field', sourceFile)}</label>
+      <label>Operator<select data-condition-field="operator">${optionsHtml(catalogArray('app_config.logic_options.operators', ['is equal to']), condition.operator)}</select></label>
+      <label>Value Mode<div class="value-mode-pills"><button type="button" class="value-mode-pill ${valueMode === 'values' ? 'active' : ''}" data-condition-set="valueMode" data-condition-value="values">Values</button><button type="button" class="value-mode-pill ${valueMode === 'field' ? 'active' : ''}" data-condition-set="valueMode" data-condition-value="field">Field</button><button type="button" class="value-mode-pill ${valueMode === 'blank' ? 'active' : ''}" data-condition-set="valueMode" data-condition-value="blank">Blank</button><button type="button" class="value-mode-pill ${valueMode === 'expression' ? 'active' : ''}" data-condition-set="valueMode" data-condition-value="expression">Expression</button><input type="hidden" data-condition-field="valueMode" value="${esc(valueMode)}"></div></label>
+      ${valueMode === 'field'
+        ? `<label>Compare Source${renderConditionPickerTrigger('source', 'compareSourceFile', condition.compareSourceFile || sourceFile, condition.compareSourceFile || sourceFile || 'Choose source')}</label><label>Compare Field${renderConditionPickerTrigger('field', 'compareFieldName', condition.compareFieldName || '', condition.compareFieldName || 'Choose field', condition.compareSourceFile || sourceFile)}</label>`
+        : valueMode === 'blank'
+          ? `<div class="logic-editor-callout">No comparison value required.</div>`
+          : valueMode === 'expression'
+            ? `<label>Expression<textarea data-condition-field="expression" placeholder="Enter expression">${esc(condition.expression || '')}</textarea></label>`
+            : `<label>Values <span class="hint inline-hint">one value per line</span><textarea data-condition-field="compareValue" class="value-list-textarea" placeholder="One value per line">${esc(valuesToLines(condition.compareValue || ''))}</textarea></label>`}
+      <button type="button" class="danger small" data-guided-delete-condition>Delete</button>
+    </div>`;
+  };
+}
+
+function valuesToLines(value) {
+  return String(value || '').split(/\r?\n|,/).map((item) => item.trim()).filter(Boolean).join('\n');
+}
+
+function renderConditionPickerTrigger(kind, fieldName, value, label, sourceFile = '') {
+  return `<div class="visual-picker-trigger-row mini-picker-trigger-row"><input type="hidden" data-condition-field="${esc(fieldName)}" value="${esc(value || '')}"><button type="button" class="visual-picker-trigger-button mini-picker-trigger" data-open-mini-picker="${kind}" data-condition-name="${esc(fieldName)}" data-picker-source="${esc(sourceFile || '')}"><span class="visual-picker-trigger-main"><strong>${esc(value || label || 'Choose...')}</strong></span><span class="visual-picker-trigger-caret">⌄</span></button></div>`;
+}
+
+function renderMiniPicker() {
+  document.querySelector('.visual-picker-modal-backdrop.dynamic-mini-picker')?.remove();
+  if (!visualPickerState) return;
+  const options = visualPickerState.kind === 'source' ? pickerSourceOptions() : pickerFieldOptions(visualPickerState.sourceFile);
+  const query = visualPickerState.query || '';
+  const filtered = options.filter((option) => `${option.sourceFile} ${option.sourceAlias} ${option.fieldName} ${option.label}`.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase()));
+  const groups = new Map();
+  filtered.forEach((option) => {
+    const group = visualPickerState.kind === 'source' ? sourceSystem({ sourceFile: option.sourceFile, alias: option.sourceAlias }) : option.sourceFile;
+    if (!groups.has(group)) groups.set(group, []);
+    groups.get(group).push(option);
+  });
+  const root = document.createElement('div');
+  root.className = 'visual-picker-modal-backdrop dynamic-mini-picker';
+  root.innerHTML = `<div class="visual-picker-modal"><div class="visual-picker-modal-header"><div><h2 class="visual-picker-modal-title">${visualPickerState.kind === 'source' ? 'Choose Source File' : 'Choose Field'}</h2><p class="visual-picker-modal-subtitle">Search grouped source-table options.</p></div><button type="button" class="visual-picker-close-button" data-close-mini-picker>×</button></div><div class="visual-picker-search-wrap"><input class="visual-picker-search-input" data-mini-picker-search value="${esc(query)}" placeholder="Search..."></div><div class="visual-picker-content">${[...groups.entries()].map(([group, items]) => `<section class="visual-picker-group"><div class="visual-picker-group-heading"><button type="button" class="visual-picker-group-toggle"><span class="visual-picker-group-title">${esc(String(group).toUpperCase())}</span><span class="visual-picker-group-count">${items.length}</span></button></div><div class="visual-picker-pill-grid">${items.map((item) => `<button type="button" class="visual-picker-pill" data-mini-picker-value="${esc(visualPickerState.kind === 'source' ? item.sourceFile : item.fieldName)}" data-mini-picker-source="${esc(item.sourceFile)}"><span class="visual-picker-pill-main">${esc(visualPickerState.kind === 'source' ? item.sourceFile : item.label)}</span><span class="visual-picker-pill-detail">${esc(visualPickerState.kind === 'source' ? item.sourceAlias : `${item.sourceFile}.${item.fieldName}`)}</span></button>`).join('')}</div></section>`).join('') || '<p class="hint">No options found.</p>'}</div></div>`;
+  document.body.append(root);
+  root.querySelector('[data-mini-picker-search]')?.focus();
+}
+
+function setPickerTargetValue(value, sourceFile = '') {
+  const target = visualPickerState?.target;
+  if (!target) return;
+  const container = target.closest('.step-card') || target.closest('.guided-condition-row');
+  if (visualPickerState.targetType === 'condition') {
+    const hidden = container?.querySelector(`[data-condition-field="${CSS.escape(visualPickerState.name)}"]`);
+    if (hidden) hidden.value = value;
+    if (visualPickerState.kind === 'field') {
+      const sourceHidden = container?.querySelector('[data-condition-field="sourceFile"]');
+      if (sourceHidden && !sourceHidden.value) sourceHidden.value = sourceFile;
+    }
+  } else {
+    const hidden = container?.querySelector(`[data-param="${CSS.escape(visualPickerState.name)}"]`);
+    if (hidden) hidden.value = value;
+    if (visualPickerState.kind === 'field') {
+      const sourceHidden = container?.querySelector('[data-param="source_file"]');
+      if (sourceHidden && !sourceHidden.value) sourceHidden.value = sourceFile;
+    }
+  }
+  visualPickerState = null;
+  document.querySelector('.visual-picker-modal-backdrop.dynamic-mini-picker')?.remove();
+  capture();
+  renderAll();
+  scheduleAutosave();
+}
+
+function moveManualStep(draggedId, targetId) {
+  if (!draggedId || !targetId || draggedId === targetId) return;
+  const steps = state.audit.steps || [];
+  const dragged = steps.find((step) => step.id === draggedId);
+  const target = steps.find((step) => step.id === targetId);
+  if (!dragged || !target || dragged.autoGeneratedReference || target.autoGeneratedReference) return;
+  const without = steps.filter((step) => step.id !== draggedId);
+  const targetIndex = without.findIndex((step) => step.id === targetId);
+  without.splice(targetIndex, 0, dragged);
+  state.audit.steps = without;
+  state.audit.steps.forEach((step, index) => { if (!step.autoGeneratedReference) step.sortOrder = index + 1; });
+  renderAll();
+  scheduleAutosave();
+}
+
+renderSteps = function renderStepsVisualCollapsible() {
+  state.audit.steps.forEach((step, index) => normalizeCatalogStep?.(step, index));
+  const entries = hierarchicalStepEntries({ includeDisabled: true });
+  e.stepsList.innerHTML = entries.map((entry) => renderStepCard(entry)).join('');
+  renderSelectedStep();
+};
+
+function renderStepTypePills(step) {
+  return `<div class="step-type-pill-row">${(STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS || []).filter(([value]) => value !== 'reference_source').map(([value, label]) => `<button type="button" class="step-type-pill ${step.step_type === value ? 'active' : ''}" data-set-step-type="${esc(value)}">${esc(label)}</button>`).join('')}<input type="hidden" data-step-type value="${esc(step.step_type)}"></div>`;
+}
+
+function renderStepCard(entry) {
+  const step = entry.step;
+  const collapsed = collapsedStepIds.has(step.id);
+  if (step.autoGeneratedReference) {
+    return `<details class="card step-card generated-reference-step ${state.selectedStepId === step.id ? 'selected' : ''}" data-id="${esc(step.id)}" ${collapsed ? '' : 'open'} data-parent-id="" style="--depth:${entry.level}"><summary class="step-summary-row"><span class="step-title"><span class="step-number">${esc(entry.number)}</span>Reference Source</span><span class="badge">Generated</span><span class="badge">Locked</span></summary><p class="hint">This reference step is generated from selected source files.</p><div class="logic-preview-small"><strong>Generated Analyst Logic</strong><p>${atlasRenderStepLogicWithPills(step)}</p></div><div class="row hierarchy-buttons"><button class="secondary small" data-select-step>Select</button></div></details>`;
+  }
+  return `<details draggable="true" class="card step-card visual-step-card ${state.selectedStepId === step.id ? 'selected' : ''} ${dropTargetLogicStepId === step.id ? 'logic-drop-target-row' : ''}" data-id="${esc(step.id)}" data-parent-id="${esc(step.parentId || '')}" ${collapsed ? '' : 'open'} style="--depth:${entry.level}"><summary class="step-summary-row"><span class="logic-drag-handle" title="Drag to reorder">⋮⋮</span><span class="step-title"><span class="step-number">${esc(entry.number)}</span>${esc((STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS || []).find(([value]) => value === step.step_type)?.[1] || step.step_type)}</span><span class="badge branch-badge">${esc(entry.branchLabel)}</span></summary><div class="hierarchy-controls"><label>Parent Step<select data-step-parent>${parentOptionsForStep(step).map((option) => `<option value="${esc(option.id)}" ${(step.parentId || '') === option.id ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(option.label)}</option>`).join('')}</select></label><label>Branch Type<select data-step-branch>${branchTypeOptions(step)}</select></label></div><input type="hidden" data-step-title value="${esc(step.title || 'Logic Step')}">${renderStepTypePills(step)}<input type="hidden" data-step-source value="${esc(step.source || '')}"><input type="hidden" data-step-field value="${esc(step.field || '')}"><input type="hidden" data-step-action value="${esc(step.action || '')}">${renderGuidedBuilder(step)}<label>Manual Notes<textarea data-step-notes>${esc(step.notes || '')}</textarea></label><label><span class="check"><input type="checkbox" data-step-enabled ${step.enabled !== false ? 'checked' : ''}> Enabled</span></label><div class="logic-preview-small"><strong>Generated Analyst Logic</strong><p>${atlasRenderStepLogicWithPills(step)}</p></div><div class="row hierarchy-buttons"><button class="secondary small" data-select-step>Select</button><button class="small" data-add-child-step>Add Child Step</button><button class="secondary small" data-indent>Indent</button><button class="secondary small" data-outdent>Outdent</button><button class="danger small" data-delete-step>Delete Step</button></div></details>`;
+}
+
+document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
+  const tab = event.target.closest('[data-source-tab]');
+  if (tab) { activeSourceLibraryTab = tab.dataset.sourceTab; renderSourceFieldBrowser(); return; }
+  const audit = event.target.closest('[data-audit-library-value]');
+  if (audit) { const select = $('auditLibrarySelect'); if (select) select.value = audit.dataset.auditLibraryValue || ''; loadSelectedLibraryAudit?.(); return; }
+  const palette = event.target.closest('[data-source-palette-color]');
+  if (palette) { const card = palette.closest('.selected-source-card'); const source = state.audit.sources.find((item) => item.id === card?.dataset.id); if (source) { source.color = palette.dataset.sourcePaletteColor; renderAll(); scheduleAutosave(); } return; }
+  const stepType = event.target.closest('[data-set-step-type]');
+  if (stepType) { const card = stepType.closest('.step-card'); const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === card?.dataset.id); if (step) { step.step_type = stepType.dataset.setStepType; step.type = legacyTypeFromStepType?.(step.step_type) || step.type; step.params = {}; normalizeCatalogStep?.(step); renderAll(); scheduleAutosave(); } return; }
+  const picker = event.target.closest('[data-open-mini-picker]');
+  if (picker) { visualPickerState = { kind: picker.dataset.openMiniPicker, name: picker.dataset.paramName || picker.dataset.conditionName, targetType: picker.dataset.conditionName ? 'condition' : 'param', sourceFile: picker.dataset.pickerSource || picker.closest('.step-card')?.querySelector('[data-param="source_file"]')?.value || '', target: picker, query: '' }; renderMiniPicker(); return; }
+  const close = event.target.closest('[data-close-mini-picker]');
+  if (close) { visualPickerState = null; document.querySelector('.visual-picker-modal-backdrop.dynamic-mini-picker')?.remove(); return; }
+  const pick = event.target.closest('[data-mini-picker-value]');
+  if (pick) { setPickerTargetValue(pick.dataset.miniPickerValue || '', pick.dataset.miniPickerSource || ''); return; }
+  const conditionSet = event.target.closest('[data-condition-set]');
+  if (conditionSet) { const row = conditionSet.closest('.guided-condition-row'); const hidden = row?.querySelector(`[data-condition-field="${CSS.escape(conditionSet.dataset.conditionSet)}"]`); if (hidden) hidden.value = conditionSet.dataset.conditionValue || ''; capture(); renderAll(); scheduleAutosave(); return; }
+});
+
+document.addEventListener('input', (event) => {
+  if (event.target.matches('[data-mini-picker-search]') && visualPickerState) { visualPickerState.query = event.target.value; renderMiniPicker(); }
+  if (event.target.matches('[data-source-custom-color]')) { const card = event.target.closest('.selected-source-card'); const source = state.audit.sources.find((item) => item.id === card?.dataset.id); if (source) { source.color = event.target.value; renderAll(); scheduleAutosave(); } }
+});
+
+document.addEventListener('toggle', (event) => {
+  const card = event.target.closest?.('.step-card');
+  if (card && event.target.matches('.step-card')) { if (event.target.open) collapsedStepIds.delete(card.dataset.id); else collapsedStepIds.add(card.dataset.id); }
+}, true);
+
+document.addEventListener('dragstart', (event) => {
+  const card = event.target.closest('.visual-step-card');
+  if (!card) return;
+  draggedLogicStepId = card.dataset.id;
+  event.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = 'move';
+  event.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain', draggedLogicStepId);
+});
+document.addEventListener('dragover', (event) => {
+  const card = event.target.closest('.visual-step-card');
+  if (!card || !draggedLogicStepId) return;
+  event.preventDefault();
+  dropTargetLogicStepId = card.dataset.id;
+  card.classList.add('logic-drop-target-row');
+});
+document.addEventListener('dragleave', (event) => {
+  const card = event.target.closest('.visual-step-card');
+  if (card) card.classList.remove('logic-drop-target-row');
+});
+document.addEventListener('drop', (event) => {
+  const card = event.target.closest('.visual-step-card');
+  if (!card) return;
+  event.preventDefault();
+  moveManualStep(draggedLogicStepId || event.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain'), card.dataset.id);
+  draggedLogicStepId = '';
+  dropTargetLogicStepId = '';
+});
+document.addEventListener('dragend', () => { draggedLogicStepId = ''; dropTargetLogicStepId = ''; });
+
+/* Spacing/data-browser refinement layer */
+let activeSelectedSourceName = '';
+
+function firstLetter(value) {
+  const c = String(value || '').trim().charAt(0).toUpperCase();
+  return /[A-Z]/.test(c) ? c : '#';
+}
+function alphaSortItems(items, getName) {
+  return [...items].sort((a, b) => String(getName(a) || '').localeCompare(String(getName(b) || ''), undefined, { sensitivity: 'base', numeric: true }));
+}
+function alphaLetters(items, getName) {
+  return [...new Set(alphaSortItems(items, getName).map((item) => firstLetter(getName(item))))];
+}
+function scrollContactsLetter(containerSelector, letter) {
+  const container = document.querySelector(containerSelector);
+  const target = container?.querySelector(`[data-alpha-letter="${CSS.escape(letter)}"]`);
+  if (container && target) container.scrollTo({ top: target.offsetTop - container.offsetTop, behavior: 'smooth' });
+}
+
+function allAuditLibraryItems() {
+  const items = [];
+  (repoLibraryState?.audits || []).forEach((audit) => items.push({ value: `repo:${audit.id}`, title: audit.name || audit.id, detail: audit.description || audit.file || '', kind: 'Repo Audit' }));
+  (state.savedFlows || []).forEach((flow) => items.push({ value: `local:${flow.id}`, title: flow.name || 'Saved audit', detail: flow.updatedAt ? `Saved ${new Date(flow.updatedAt).toLocaleString()}` : 'Saved in this browser', kind: 'Saved Audit' }));
+  if (typeof workbookState !== 'undefined' && workbookState.loaded) (workbookState.auditSheets || []).forEach((name) => items.push({ value: `workbook:${name}`, title: name, detail: 'Workbook audit sheet', kind: 'Workbook Import' }));
+  return alphaSortItems(items, (item) => item.title);
+}
+
+renderAuditLibrarySelect = function renderAuditLibraryContacts() {
+  const select = $('auditLibrarySelect');
+  const visual = $('auditLibraryVisual');
+  const allItems = allAuditLibraryItems();
+  const currentSearch = $('auditLibrarySearch')?.value || '';
+  const query = currentSearch.toLowerCase().trim();
+  const items = allItems.filter((item) => `${item.title} ${item.detail} ${item.kind}`.toLowerCase().includes(query));
+
+  if (select) {
+    select.innerHTML = allItems.length
+      ? allItems.map((item) => `<option value="${esc(item.value)}">${esc(item.title)}</option>`).join('')
+      : '<option value="">No audits available</option>';
+  }
+
+  if (!visual) return;
+
+  const letters = alphaLetters(items, (item) => item.title);
+  const rows = items.length
+    ? items.map((item, idx) => {
+      const letter = firstLetter(item.title);
+      const previousLetter = idx ? firstLetter(items[idx - 1].title) : '';
+      const header = letter !== previousLetter
+        ? `<div class="contacts-letter-header audit-letter-header" data-alpha-letter="${esc(letter)}">${esc(letter)}</div>`
+        : '';
+      return `${header}<button type="button" class="contacts-row audit-contact-row" data-audit-library-value="${esc(item.value)}"><span class="audit-contact-content"><span class="contacts-title audit-contact-title">${esc(item.title)}</span><span class="contacts-detail audit-contact-detail">${esc(item.kind)}${item.detail ? ` · ${esc(item.detail)}` : ''}</span></span></button>`;
+    }).join('')
+    : '<p class="hint contacts-empty audit-contacts-empty">No audits match the current search.</p>';
+
+  visual.innerHTML = `<div class="contacts-menu-header audit-library-search-row"><input id="auditLibrarySearch" value="${esc(currentSearch)}" placeholder="Search audits..."><button type="button" class="small" data-new-audit-from-library>+ New Audit</button></div><div class="contacts-list-shell audit-library-shell"><div class="contacts-list audit-library-list" data-contacts-list="audit">${rows}</div><div class="contacts-letter-index audit-letter-index">${letters.map((letter) => `<button type="button" data-scroll-letter="${esc(letter)}" data-scroll-scope="audit">${esc(letter)}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>`;
+};
+
+function filteredSourceEntries() {
+  const tab = activeSourceLibraryTab || 'all';
+  const query = ($('sourceFieldSearch')?.value || '').toLowerCase().trim();
+  const selected = selectedSourceSet();
+  return alphaSortItems(sourceCatalogEntries(), (source) => source.sourceFile).filter((source) => {
+    if (tab === 'selected' && !selected.has(source.sourceFile)) return false;
+    if (tab !== 'all' && tab !== 'selected' && sourceSystem(source) !== tab) return false;
+    return sourceMatchesSearch(source, query);
+  });
+}
+
+renderSourceFieldBrowser = function renderSourceFieldBrowserContacts() {
+  const panel = $('sourceFieldBrowser');
+  const tabRoot = $('sourceLibraryTabs');
+  if (!panel) return;
+  const allSources = sourceCatalogEntries();
+  const selected = selectedSourceSet();
+  const tabs = [
+    ['all', 'All', allSources],
+    ['workday', 'Workday', allSources.filter((source) => sourceSystem(source) === 'workday')],
+    ['sap', 'SAP', allSources.filter((source) => sourceSystem(source) === 'sap')],
+    ['other', 'Other / Unknown', allSources.filter((source) => sourceSystem(source) === 'other')],
+    ['selected', 'Selected', allSources.filter((source) => selected.has(source.sourceFile))],
+  ];
+  if (tabRoot) tabRoot.innerHTML = tabs.map(([key, label, items]) => `<button type="button" class="source-library-tab ${activeSourceLibraryTab === key ? 'active' : ''}" data-source-tab="${key}">${esc(label)} <span>${items.length}</span></button>`).join('');
+  const sources = filteredSourceEntries();
+  const letters = alphaLetters(sources, (source) => source.sourceFile);
+  panel.innerHTML = `<div class="contacts-list-shell source-contacts-shell"><div class="contacts-list" data-contacts-list="source">${sources.length ? sources.map((source, idx) => { const letter = firstLetter(source.sourceFile); const prev = idx ? firstLetter(sources[idx - 1].sourceFile) : ''; const header = letter !== prev ? `<div class="contacts-letter-header" data-alpha-letter="${esc(letter)}">${esc(letter)}</div>` : ''; const isSelected = selected.has(source.sourceFile); return `${header}<div class="contacts-row source-contact-row ${isSelected ? 'selected' : ''}"><button type="button" class="contacts-row-main" data-source-detail-name="${esc(source.sourceFile)}"><span class="contacts-title">${esc(source.sourceFile)}</span><span class="contacts-detail">${esc(source.alias || source.sourceFile)} · ${sourceSystem(source).toUpperCase()} · ${source.fields?.length || 0} fields</span></button><button type="button" class="small ${isSelected ? 'secondary' : ''}" data-add-source-to-audit="${esc(source.sourceFile)}">${isSelected ? 'Refresh' : 'Use'}</button></div>`; }).join('') : '<p class="hint contacts-empty">No source files match the current search.</p>'}</div><div class="contacts-letter-index">${letters.map((letter) => `<button type="button" data-scroll-letter="${esc(letter)}" data-scroll-scope="source">${esc(letter)}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>`;
+};

+function fieldRowsForSource(sourceName) {
+  return fieldsForSource(sourceName).map((field, index) => {
+    const raw = field.fieldName || field.rawFieldName || field.name || field.label || '';
+    const vanity = field.vanityLabel || field.label || (field.name !== raw ? field.name : '') || '';
+    return {
+      order: field.order || index + 1,
+      fieldName: raw,
+      vanityLabel: vanity,
+      formatting: field.formatting || field.format || field.formattingNotes || '',
+      displayed: field.displayed || field.display || '',
+      used: field.used || field.usedInAudit || '',
+    };
+  });
+}
+function renderSelectedSourceColorControl(source) {
+  const color = source.color || SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE[0];
+  return `<details class="compact-color-picker"><summary><span class="single-color-swatch" style="background:${esc(color)}"></span><span>${esc(color)}</span></summary><div class="compact-color-menu">${SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE.map((paletteColor) => `<button type="button" class="color-swatch ${paletteColor === color ? 'active' : ''}" data-source-palette-color="${esc(paletteColor)}" style="background:${esc(paletteColor)}" title="${esc(paletteColor)}"></button>`).join('')}<label class="custom-color-label">Custom <input type="color" data-source-custom-color value="${esc(color)}"></label></div></details>`;
+}
+function renderSelectedSourceTable(sourceName) {
+  if (!sourceName) return '<p class="hint">Select a source to view its fields.</p>';
+  const rows = fieldRowsForSource(sourceName);
+  const source = (state.audit.sources || []).find((item) => item.name === sourceName);
+  if (!source) return '<p class="hint">Select a source to view its fields.</p>';
+  return `<section class="selected-source-data-view"><div class="title-row"><div><h4>${esc(source.name)}</h4><p class="hint">${esc(source.alias || sourceAliasFor?.(source.name) || source.name)} · ${rows.length} fields</p></div><button type="button" class="secondary small" data-close-source-data>Back to selected sources</button></div><div class="selected-source-table-wrap"><table class="selected-source-table"><thead><tr><th>#</th><th>Field Name</th><th>Vanity Label</th><th>Formatting</th><th>Used</th><th>Displayed</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map((row) => `<tr><td>${esc(row.order)}</td><td>${esc(row.fieldName)}</td><td>${esc(row.vanityLabel)}</td><td>${esc(row.formatting)}</td><td>${esc(row.used)}</td><td>${esc(row.displayed)}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div></section>`;
+}
+renderSources = function renderCombinedSelectedSources() {
+  const target = e.sourcesList;
+  const sources = state.audit.sources || [];
+  if (!sources.length) {
+    target.innerHTML = '<p class="hint">No source files selected yet. Use the Source Library above to add files to this audit.</p>';
+    return;
+  }
+  if (activeSelectedSourceName && !sources.some((source) => source.name === activeSelectedSourceName)) activeSelectedSourceName = '';
+  if (activeSelectedSourceName) {
+    target.innerHTML = renderSelectedSourceTable(activeSelectedSourceName);
+    return;
+  }
+  target.innerHTML = `<div class="selected-source-list">${sources.map((source) => { const rows = fieldRowsForSource(source.name); return `<div class="selected-source-compact-card" data-id="${esc(source.id)}" style="--source-color:${esc(source.color || SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE[0])}"><button type="button" class="selected-source-main" data-source-detail-name="${esc(source.name)}"><span class="single-color-swatch" style="background:${esc(source.color || SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE[0])}"></span><span><strong>${esc(source.name)}</strong><small>${esc(source.alias || sourceAliasFor?.(source.name) || source.name)} · ${rows.length} fields</small></span></button>${renderSelectedSourceColorControl(source)}<button type="button" class="danger small" data-remove-selected-source="${esc(source.name)}">Remove</button></div>`; }).join('')}</div>`;
+};

+/* Logic Builder mockup alignment override */
+function stepTypeLabel(step) {
+  return (STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS || []).find(([value]) => value === step.step_type)?.[1] || step.step_type || 'Logic Step';
+}
+function renderLiveSourceOptions(selected = '') {
+  return ['<option value="">Select source file...</option>', ...sourceCatalogEntries().map((source) => `<option value="${esc(source.sourceFile)}" ${selected === source.sourceFile ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(source.sourceFile)}${source.alias && source.alias !== source.sourceFile ? ` (${esc(source.alias)})` : ''}</option>`)].join('');
+}
+function renderLiveFieldOptions(sourceFile = '', selected = '') {
+  const fields = fieldsForSource(sourceFile);
+  return ['<option value="">Select field...</option>', ...fields.map((field) => {
+    const raw = field.fieldName || field.name || field.label || '';
+    const vanity = field.vanityLabel || field.label || raw;
+    return `<option value="${esc(raw)}" ${selected === raw || selected === vanity ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(raw)}${vanity && vanity !== raw ? ` (${esc(vanity)})` : ''}</option>`;
+  })].join('');
+}
+function conditionsForLiveEditor(step) {
+  const conditions = parseStepConditions?.(step) || [];
+  if (conditions.length) return conditions;
+  return [defaultGuidedCondition?.(step) || { id: uid('cond'), connector: 'IF', sourceFile: step.source || step.params?.source_file || '', sourceAlias: '', fieldName: step.field || step.params?.field_name || '', operator: 'is equal to', valueMode: 'values', compareValue: '' }];
+}
+function writeLiveConditions(step, conditions) {
+  step.params = step.params || {};
+  step.conditions = conditions.map((condition) => ({ id: condition.id || uid('cond'), field: condition.fieldName || condition.field || '', operator: condition.operator || 'is equal to', value: condition.compareValue || condition.value || '' }));
+  step.params.conditions_json = JSON.stringify(conditions);
+}
+function renderLiveCriteriaEditor(step) {
+  if (!step || step.autoGeneratedReference) {
+    return '<p class="hint">Generated reference steps are controlled by the selected source files. Remove or add source files from the Fields page to change generated reference steps.</p>';
+  }
+  normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+  const p = step.params || {};
+  const firstCondition = conditionsForLiveEditor(step)[0] || {};
+  const activeSource = p.source_file || firstCondition.sourceFile || step.source || '';
+  const activeField = p.field_name || firstCondition.fieldName || step.field || '';
+  return `<div class="live-criteria-editor">
+    <div class="field-row full"><span>Step Type</span><div class="step-type-pill-row live-step-type-row">${(STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS || []).filter(([value]) => value !== 'reference_source').map(([value, label]) => `<button type="button" class="step-type-pill ${step.step_type === value ? 'active' : ''}" data-live-step-type="${esc(value)}">${esc(label)}</button>`).join('')}</div></div>
+    <label class="field-row"><span>Source File</span><select data-live-param="source_file">${renderLiveSourceOptions(activeSource)}</select></label>
+    <label class="field-row"><span>Field Name</span><select data-live-param="field_name">${renderLiveFieldOptions(activeSource, activeField)}</select></label>
+    <label class="field-row"><span>Operator</span><select data-live-condition="operator">${optionsHtml(catalogArray('app_config.logic_options.operators', ['is equal to','is not equal to','contains','does not contain','is blank/null','is not blank/null']), firstCondition.operator || p.operator || 'is equal to')}</select></label>
+    <label class="field-row full"><span>Compare Values <em class="small">one value per line</em></span><textarea data-live-condition="compareValue" class="value-list-textarea" placeholder="One value per line">${esc(valuesToLines(firstCondition.compareValue || p.compare_value || ''))}</textarea></label>
+    <label class="field-row full"><span>THEN / Action Text</span><textarea data-live-param="then_action" placeholder="Describe the action for this step">${esc(p.then_action || step.action || '')}</textarea></label>
+  </div>`;
+}

+renderSelectedStep = function renderSelectedStepLiveOutput() {
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === state.selectedStepId);
+  const target = e.selectedStepDetails || $('selectedStepDetails');
+  if (!target) return;
+  if (!step) {
+    target.innerHTML = '<p class="hint">Select or create a step to edit criteria and preview standardized output.</p>';
+    setText?.('selectedRuleBadge', 'No rule selected');
+    return;
+  }
+  const number = stepNumberFor(step.id) || '?';
+  setText?.('selectedRuleBadge', `Step ${number}`);
+  target.innerHTML = `<div class="live-output-heading"><div><span class="badge blue">Step ${esc(number)}</span><strong>${esc(stepTypeLabel(step))}</strong></div><span class="small">${step.autoGeneratedReference ? 'Generated reference step' : 'Editable rule'}</span></div><div class="callout logic-output-box"><strong>Generated Analyst Logic</strong><div class="logic-output-text">${atlasRenderStepLogicWithPills(step)}</div></div>${renderLiveCriteriaEditor(step)}`;
+};
+
+function renderLogicTablePreviewRows() {
+  const target = $('logicBuilderTableBody');
+  if (!target) return;
+  const entries = hierarchicalStepEntries({ includeDisabled: true });
+  target.innerHTML = entries.length ? entries.map((entry) => `<tr class="${state.selectedStepId === entry.step.id ? 'selected-row' : ''}" data-table-step-id="${esc(entry.step.id)}"><td>${esc(entry.number)}</td><td>${esc(stepTypeLabel(entry.step))}</td><td>${atlasRenderStepLogicWithPills(entry.step)}</td><td>${entry.step.enabled === false ? '<span class="badge red">Disabled</span>' : '<span class="badge blue">Active</span>'}</td></tr>`).join('') : '<tr><td colspan="4" class="hint">No logic steps yet.</td></tr>';
+}

+renderSteps = function renderStepsMockupAligned() {
+  state.audit.steps.forEach((step, index) => normalizeCatalogStep?.(step, index));
+  const entries = hierarchicalStepEntries({ includeDisabled: true });
+  e.stepsList.innerHTML = entries.map((entry) => renderLogicRuleCard(entry)).join('');
+  renderSelectedStep();
+  renderLogicTablePreviewRows();
+};
+
+function renderLogicRuleCard(entry) {
+  const step = entry.step;
+  const collapsed = collapsedStepIds.has(step.id);
+  const selected = state.selectedStepId === step.id;
+  const generated = !!step.autoGeneratedReference;
+  const analyst = analystLogic(step);
+  if (generated) {
+    return `<details class="rule-card step-card generated-reference-step ${selected ? 'selected' : ''}" data-id="${esc(step.id)}" data-parent-id="" ${collapsed ? '' : 'open'} style="--depth:${entry.level}">
+      <summary class="rule-head step-summary-row"><div><span class="step-number">${esc(entry.number)}</span><strong>Reference Source</strong><div class="step-meta"><span class="badge blue">Generated</span><span class="badge blue">Locked</span></div></div><button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-select-step>Select</button></summary>
+      <div class="callout logic-output-box">${renderLogicWithPills(analyst)}</div>
+    </details>`;
+  }
+  return `<details draggable="true" class="rule-card step-card visual-step-card ${selected ? 'selected' : ''} ${dropTargetLogicStepId === step.id ? 'drop-hint' : ''}" data-id="${esc(step.id)}" data-parent-id="${esc(step.parentId || '')}" ${collapsed ? '' : 'open'} style="--depth:${entry.level}">
+    <summary class="rule-head step-summary-row"><div class="rule-title-wrap"><span class="drag-handle" title="Drag to reorder">⋮⋮</span><span class="step-number">${esc(entry.number)}</span><strong>${esc(stepTypeLabel(step))}</strong><div class="step-meta"><span class="badge blue">${esc(entry.branchLabel)}</span><span class="badge green">Editable</span></div></div><button type="button" class="btn ghost small" data-select-step>Select</button></summary>
+    <div class="logic-rule-body"><div class="callout logic-output-box">${renderLogicWithPills(analyst)}</div><div class="logic-rule-actions"><button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-add-child-step>Add Child</button><button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-indent>Indent</button><button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-outdent>Outdent</button><button type="button" class="btn danger small" data-delete-step>Delete</button></div><div class="step-card-fields">${renderGuidedBuilder(step)}<label>Manual Notes<textarea data-step-notes>${esc(step.notes || '')}</textarea></label><label><span class="check"><input type="checkbox" data-step-enabled ${step.enabled !== false ? 'checked' : ''}> Enabled</span></label><input type="hidden" data-step-title value="${esc(step.title || 'Logic Step')}"><input type="hidden" data-step-type value="${esc(step.step_type)}"><input type="hidden" data-step-source value="${esc(step.source || '')}"><input type="hidden" data-step-field value="${esc(step.field || '')}"><input type="hidden" data-step-action value="${esc(step.action || '')}"></div></div>
+  </details>`;
+}
+
+document.addEventListener('change', (event) => {
+  const liveParam = event.target.closest('[data-live-param]');
+  const liveCondition = event.target.closest('[data-live-condition]');
+  if (!liveParam && !liveCondition) return;
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === state.selectedStepId);
+  if (!step || step.autoGeneratedReference) return;
+  step.params = step.params || {};
+  if (liveParam) {
+    const key = liveParam.dataset.liveParam;
+    step.params[key] = liveParam.value;
+    if (key === 'source_file') {
+      step.source = liveParam.value;
+      step.params.source_alias = sourceAliasFor?.(liveParam.value) || liveParam.value;
+      const conditions = conditionsForLiveEditor(step);
+      conditions[0].sourceFile = liveParam.value;
+      conditions[0].sourceAlias = step.params.source_alias;
+      writeLiveConditions(step, conditions);
+    }
+    if (key === 'field_name') {
+      step.field = liveParam.value;
+      const conditions = conditionsForLiveEditor(step);
+      conditions[0].fieldName = liveParam.value;
+      writeLiveConditions(step, conditions);
+    }
+  }
+  if (liveCondition) {
+    const key = liveCondition.dataset.liveCondition;
+    const conditions = conditionsForLiveEditor(step);
+    conditions[0][key] = key === 'compareValue' ? valuesToLines(liveCondition.value) : liveCondition.value;
+    writeLiveConditions(step, conditions);
+    if (key === 'compareValue') step.params.compare_value = valuesToLines(liveCondition.value);
+    if (key === 'operator') step.params.operator = liveCondition.value;
+  }
+  normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+  renderAll();
+  scheduleAutosave();
+}, true);
+
+document.addEventListener('input', (event) => {
+  const liveField = event.target.closest('[data-live-param="then_action"], [data-live-condition="compareValue"]');
+  if (!liveField) return;
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === state.selectedStepId);
+  if (!step || step.autoGeneratedReference) return;
+  step.params = step.params || {};
+  if (liveField.dataset.liveParam === 'then_action') {
+    step.params.then_action = liveField.value;
+    step.action = liveField.value;
+  }
+  if (liveField.dataset.liveCondition === 'compareValue') {
+    const conditions = conditionsForLiveEditor(step);
+    conditions[0].compareValue = valuesToLines(liveField.value);
+    writeLiveConditions(step, conditions);
+    step.params.compare_value = valuesToLines(liveField.value);
+  }
+  renderSelectedStep();
+  renderPreview();
+  renderLogicTablePreviewRows();
+  scheduleAutosave();
+}, true);
+
+document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
+  const stepTypeButton = event.target.closest('[data-live-step-type]');
+  if (stepTypeButton) {
+    const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === state.selectedStepId);
+    if (!step || step.autoGeneratedReference) return;
+    step.step_type = stepTypeButton.dataset.liveStepType;
+    step.type = legacyTypeFromStepType?.(step.step_type) || step.type;
+    step.params = {};
+    normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+    renderAll();
+    scheduleAutosave();
+    return;
+  }
+  const tableRow = event.target.closest('[data-table-step-id]');
+  if (tableRow) {
+    state.selectedStepId = tableRow.dataset.tableStepId;
+    renderSteps();
+    renderSelectedStep();
+  }
+});
