+
+/* Logic Builder selected-step editor redesign */
+function atlasStepTypeLabel(step) {
+  return (STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS || []).find(([value]) => value === step.step_type)?.[1] || step.step_type || 'Logic Step';
+}
+function atlasSelectOptions(options, selected) {
+  return options.map((option) => Array.isArray(option)
+    ? `<option value="${esc(option[0])}" ${selected === option[0] ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(option[1])}</option>`
+    : `<option value="${esc(option)}" ${selected === option ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(option)}</option>`).join('');
+}
+function atlasVisibleStepEntries() {
+  return hierarchicalStepEntries({ includeDisabled: true });
+}
+function atlasEditorValue(root, selector, fallback = '') {
+  const field = root.querySelector(selector);
+  if (!field) return fallback;
+  if (field.type === 'checkbox') return field.checked;
+  return field.value;
+}
+function atlasCaptureGuidedFields(root, step) {
+  normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+  step.params = step.params && typeof step.params === 'object' ? step.params : {};
+  root.querySelectorAll('[data-param]').forEach((input) => {
+    const key = input.dataset.param;
+    if (!key) return;
+    step.params[key] = input.type === 'checkbox' ? (input.checked ? 'Yes' : 'No') : input.value;
+  });
+  const conditionRows = [...root.querySelectorAll('.guided-condition-row')];
+  if (conditionRows.length) {
+    const conditions = conditionRows.map((row, index) => {
+      const get = (field) => row.querySelector(`[data-condition-field="${field}"]`)?.value || '';
+      const sourceFile = get('sourceFile');
+      const compareSourceFile = get('compareSourceFile') || sourceFile;
+      return {
+        id: row.dataset.conditionId || uid('cond'),
+        connector: index === 0 ? 'IF' : (get('connector') || 'AND'),
+        conditionType: 'row_compare',
+        sourceFile,
+        sourceAlias: sourceAliasFor(sourceFile),
+        fieldName: get('fieldName'),
+        operator: get('operator') || 'is equal to',
+        valueMode: get('valueMode') || 'values',
+        compareValue: get('compareValue'),
+        compareSourceFile,
+        compareSourceAlias: sourceAliasFor(compareSourceFile),
+        compareFieldName: get('compareFieldName'),
+        expression: get('expression'),
+      };
+    });
+    step.params.conditions_json = JSON.stringify(conditions);
+    step.conditions = conditions.map((condition) => ({
+      id: condition.id,
+      field: condition.fieldName,
+      operator: condition.operator,
+      value: condition.compareValue,
+    }));
+  }
+  if (step.params.source_file) {
+    step.source = step.params.source_file;
+    step.params.source_alias = sourceAliasFor(step.params.source_file);
+  }
+  if (step.params.field_name) step.field = step.params.field_name;
+  if (step.params.column_name) step.field = step.params.column_name;
+}
+function atlasCommitLiveEditor({ rerender = false } = {}) {
+  const root = document.querySelector('[data-live-editor-step-id]');
+  if (!root) return;
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === root.dataset.liveEditorStepId);
+  if (!step || step.autoGeneratedReference) return;
+  const nextType = atlasEditorValue(root, '[data-live-step-type-select]', step.step_type || 'if_then');
+  const typeChanged = nextType && nextType !== step.step_type;
+  step.step_type = nextType;
+  step.type = legacyTypeFromStepType?.(step.step_type) || step.type;
+  if (typeChanged) {
+    step.params = {};
+    normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+    renderAll();
+    scheduleAutosave();
+    return;
+  }
+  step.title = atlasEditorValue(root, '[data-live-step-title]', step.title || atlasStepTypeLabel(step));
+  const parentId = atlasEditorValue(root, '[data-live-parent]', step.parentId || '') || null;
+  if ((parentId || null) !== (step.parentId || null)) setStepParent(step, parentId);
+  step.branchType = step.parentId ? atlasEditorValue(root, '[data-live-branch]', step.branchType || 'then') : 'root';
+  step.enabled = !!atlasEditorValue(root, '[data-live-enabled]', step.enabled !== false);
+  step.notes = atlasEditorValue(root, '[data-step-notes-live]', step.notes || '');
+  atlasCaptureGuidedFields(root, step);
+  normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+  if (rerender) {
+    renderAll();
+  } else {
+    const output = root.querySelector('[data-live-generated-output]');
+    if (output) output.innerHTML = atlasRenderStepLogicWithPills(step);
+    renderPreview?.();
+    renderLogicTablePreviewRows?.();
+  }
+  scheduleAutosave();
+}
+function atlasRenderParentOptions(step) {
+  return parentOptionsForStep(step).map((option) => `<option value="${esc(option.id)}" ${(step.parentId || '') === option.id ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(option.label)}</option>`).join('');
+}
+function atlasRenderBranchEditor(step) {
+  if (!step.parentId) return '<input type="hidden" data-live-branch value="root"><p class="hint">Top-level rule. Use Indent to nest it under the previous rule.</p>';
+  return `<label><span>Branch Type</span><select data-live-branch>${branchTypeOptions(step)}</select></label>`;
+}
+function atlasRenderStepEditor(step) {
+  if (!step || step.autoGeneratedReference) {
+    return '<div class="logic-editor-section"><p class="hint">Generated reference steps are controlled by selected source files. Select a manual rule to edit its logic criteria.</p></div>';
+  }
+  normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+  return `<div class="logic-editor-form" data-live-editor-step-id="${esc(step.id)}">
+    <section class="logic-editor-section">
+      <h3>Step settings</h3>
+      <div class="logic-editor-grid">
+        <label class="full"><span>Step Name</span><input data-live-step-title value="${esc(step.title || atlasStepTypeLabel(step))}" placeholder="Name this rule"></label>
+        <label><span>Step Type</span><select data-live-step-type-select>${atlasSelectOptions((STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS || []).filter(([value]) => value !== 'reference_source'), step.step_type)}</select></label>
+        <label><span>Parent Step</span><select data-live-parent>${atlasRenderParentOptions(step)}</select></label>
+        ${atlasRenderBranchEditor(step)}
+        <label><span class="check"><input type="checkbox" data-live-enabled ${step.enabled !== false ? 'checked' : ''}> Enabled</span></label>
+      </div>
+      <div class="logic-editor-toolbar">
+        <button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-indent>Indent</button>
+        <button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-outdent>Outdent</button>
+        <button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-add-child-step>Add Child</button>
+        <button type="button" class="btn danger small" data-delete-step>Delete</button>
+      </div>
+    </section>
+    <section class="logic-editor-section">
+      <h3>Criteria</h3>
+      ${renderGuidedBuilder(step)}
+    </section>
+    <section class="logic-editor-section">
+      <h3>Notes</h3>
+      <label class="full"><span>Manual Notes</span><textarea data-step-notes-live placeholder="Notes for this step">${esc(step.notes || '')}</textarea></label>
+    </section>
+  </div>`;
+}
+renderSelectedStep = function renderSelectedStepEditorPane() {
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === state.selectedStepId);
+  const target = e.selectedStepDetails || $('selectedStepDetails');
+  if (!target) return;
+  if (!step) {
+    target.innerHTML = '<p class="hint">Select or create a rule to edit step criteria.</p>';
+    setText?.('selectedRuleBadge', 'No rule selected');
+    return;
+  }
+  const number = stepNumberFor(step.id) || '?';
+  setText?.('selectedRuleBadge', `Step ${number}`);
+  target.innerHTML = `<div class="logic-editor-header"><div class="logic-editor-header-main"><span class="badge blue">Step ${esc(number)}</span><strong>${esc(step.title || atlasStepTypeLabel(step))}</strong><span class="small">${esc(atlasStepTypeLabel(step))} · ${step.autoGeneratedReference ? 'Generated reference step' : 'Editable rule'}</span></div></div><div class="callout logic-editor-generated"><strong>Live Standardised Output</strong><div class="logic-output-text" data-live-generated-output>${atlasRenderStepLogicWithPills(step)}</div></div>${atlasRenderStepEditor(step)}`;
+};
+function renderLogicTablePreviewRows() {
+  const target = $('logicBuilderTableBody');
+  if (!target) return;
+  const entries = atlasVisibleStepEntries();
+  target.innerHTML = entries.length ? entries.map((entry) => `<tr class="${state.selectedStepId === entry.step.id ? 'selected-row' : ''}" data-table-step-id="${esc(entry.step.id)}"><td>${esc(entry.number)}</td><td>${esc(atlasStepTypeLabel(entry.step))}</td><td>${atlasRenderStepLogicWithPills(entry.step)}</td><td>${entry.step.enabled === false ? '<span class="badge red">Disabled</span>' : '<span class="badge blue">Active</span>'}</td></tr>`).join('') : '<tr><td colspan="4" class="hint">No logic steps yet.</td></tr>';
+}
+function atlasRenderHiddenPreserveFields(step) {
+  return `<div class="logic-rule-hidden-fields"><input type="hidden" data-step-parent value="${esc(step.parentId || '')}"><input type="hidden" data-step-branch value="${esc(step.branchType || (step.parentId ? 'then' : 'root'))}"><input type="hidden" data-step-title value="${esc(step.title || atlasStepTypeLabel(step))}"><input type="hidden" data-step-type value="${esc(step.step_type || '')}"><input type="hidden" data-step-source value="${esc(step.source || '')}"><input type="hidden" data-step-field value="${esc(step.field || '')}"><input type="hidden" data-step-action value="${esc(step.action || '')}"><textarea data-step-notes>${esc(step.notes || '')}</textarea><input type="checkbox" data-step-enabled ${step.enabled !== false ? 'checked' : ''}></div>`;
+}
+function renderLogicRuleCard(entry) {
+  const step = entry.step;
+  const selected = state.selectedStepId === step.id;
+  const generated = !!step.autoGeneratedReference;
+  const cardClass = `logic-rule-card step-card ${generated ? 'generated-reference-step' : 'visual-step-card'} ${selected ? 'selected' : ''}`;
+  const draggable = generated ? '' : ' draggable="true"';
+  const title = generated ? 'Reference Source' : atlasStepTypeLabel(step);
+  const status = step.enabled === false ? '<span class="badge red">Disabled</span>' : generated ? '<span class="badge blue">Generated</span>' : '<span class="badge green">Editable</span>';
+  return `<div class="${cardClass}" data-id="${esc(step.id)}" data-parent-id="${esc(step.parentId || '')}" style="--depth:${entry.level}"${draggable}>
+    <div class="logic-rule-title-row">
+      <div class="logic-rule-title-main"><span class="drag-handle" title="Drag to reorder">⋮⋮</span><span class="step-number">${esc(entry.number)}</span><span class="logic-rule-title-text"><strong>${esc(title)}</strong><span class="small">${esc(entry.branchLabel)}${step.title && step.title !== title ? ` · ${esc(step.title)}` : ''}</span></span></div>
+      <div class="step-meta">${status}</div>
+    </div>
+    <div class="logic-rule-summary">${atlasRenderStepLogicWithPills(step)}</div>
+    <div class="logic-rule-actions">
+      <button type="button" class="btn ghost small" data-select-step>Edit</button>
+      ${generated ? '' : '<button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-indent>Indent</button><button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-outdent>Outdent</button><button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-add-child-step>Add Child</button><button type="button" class="btn danger small" data-delete-step>Delete</button>'}
+    </div>
+    ${atlasRenderHiddenPreserveFields(step)}
+  </div>`;
+}
+renderSteps = function renderStepsEditorRedesign() {
+  state.audit.steps.forEach((step, index) => normalizeCatalogStep?.(step, index));
+  const entries = atlasVisibleStepEntries();
+  e.stepsList.innerHTML = entries.map((entry) => renderLogicRuleCard(entry)).join('');
+  renderSelectedStep();
+  renderLogicTablePreviewRows();
+};
+const atlasPreviousSetPickerTargetValue = typeof setPickerTargetValue === 'function' ? setPickerTargetValue : null;
+setPickerTargetValue = function setPickerTargetValueEditorAware(value, sourceFile = '') {
+  const target = visualPickerState?.target;
+  if (!target) return;
+  const container = target.closest('.step-card') || target.closest('.guided-condition-row') || target.closest('[data-live-editor-step-id]');
+  if (visualPickerState.targetType === 'condition') {
+    const hidden = container?.querySelector(`[data-condition-field="${CSS.escape(visualPickerState.name)}"]`);
+    if (hidden) hidden.value = value;
+    if (visualPickerState.kind === 'field') {
+      const sourceHidden = container?.querySelector('[data-condition-field="sourceFile"]');
+      if (sourceHidden && !sourceHidden.value) sourceHidden.value = sourceFile;
+    }
+  } else {
+    const hidden = container?.querySelector(`[data-param="${CSS.escape(visualPickerState.name)}"]`);
+    if (hidden) hidden.value = value;
+    if (visualPickerState.kind === 'field') {
+      const sourceHidden = container?.querySelector('[data-param="source_file"]');
+      if (sourceHidden && !sourceHidden.value) sourceHidden.value = sourceFile;
+    }
+  }
+  visualPickerState = null;
+  document.querySelector('.visual-picker-modal-backdrop.dynamic-mini-picker')?.remove();
+  atlasCommitLiveEditor({ rerender: true });
+};
+document.addEventListener('change', (event) => {
+  const root = event.target.closest('[data-live-editor-step-id]');
+  if (!root) return;
+  atlasCommitLiveEditor({ rerender: event.target.matches('[data-live-step-type-select], [data-live-parent], [data-live-branch], [data-param="source_file"], [data-condition-field="sourceFile"], [data-condition-field="valueMode"]') });
+}, true);
+document.addEventListener('input', (event) => {
+  const root = event.target.closest('[data-live-editor-step-id]');
+  if (!root) return;
+  atlasCommitLiveEditor({ rerender: false });
+}, true);
+document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
+  const card = event.target.closest('.logic-rule-card');
+  if (card && !event.target.closest('button, input, select, textarea, a')) {
+    state.selectedStepId = card.dataset.id;
+    renderSteps();
+    return;
+  }
+  const tableRow = event.target.closest('[data-table-step-id]');
+  if (tableRow) {
+    state.selectedStepId = tableRow.dataset.tableStepId;
+    renderSteps();
+  }
+}, true);
+
+/* Compact Rules + selected-source editor/picker refinement */
+let atlasFieldPickerSortMode = 'source';
+
+function atlasSelectedAuditSources() {
+  const selectedNames = new Set((state.audit.sources || []).map((source) => source.name));
+  return sourceCatalogEntries().filter((source) => selectedNames.has(source.sourceFile));
+}
+
+function atlasSelectedSourceColor(sourceFile) {
+  return (state.audit.sources || []).find((source) => source.name === sourceFile)?.color || SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE?.[0] || '#4d6fa9';
+}
+
+function atlasSelectedSourceAlias(sourceFile) {
+  return (state.audit.sources || []).find((source) => source.name === sourceFile)?.alias || sourceAliasFor?.(sourceFile) || sourceFile;
+}
+
+function atlasSourceOptionsHtml(selected = '') {
+  const sources = atlasSelectedAuditSources();
+  if (!sources.length) return '<option value="">Select sources on the Fields page first...</option>';
+  return ['<option value="">Select source file...</option>', ...sources.map((source) => `<option value="${esc(source.sourceFile)}" ${selected === source.sourceFile ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(source.sourceFile)}${source.alias && source.alias !== source.sourceFile ? ` (${esc(source.alias)})` : ''}</option>`)].join('');
+}
+
+function atlasFieldOptionsHtml(sourceFile = '', selected = '') {
+  const sources = sourceFile ? atlasSelectedAuditSources().filter((source) => source.sourceFile === sourceFile) : atlasSelectedAuditSources();
+  const fields = sources.flatMap((source) => (source.fields || []).map((field) => ({ source, field })));
+  if (!fields.length) return '<option value="">Select a selected source first...</option>';
+  return ['<option value="">Select field...</option>', ...fields.map(({ source, field }) => {
+    const raw = field.fieldName || field.name || field.label || '';
+    const vanity = field.vanityLabel || field.label || raw;
+    const optionLabel = sourceFile ? `${raw}${vanity && vanity !== raw ? ` (${vanity})` : ''}` : `${source.sourceFile}.${raw}${vanity && vanity !== raw ? ` (${vanity})` : ''}`;
+    return `<option value="${esc(raw)}" data-source-file="${esc(source.sourceFile)}" ${selected === raw || selected === vanity ? 'selected' : ''}>${esc(optionLabel)}</option>`;
+  })].join('');
+}
+
+if (typeof sourceInput === 'function') {
+  sourceInput = function atlasSelectedSourceInput(name, value, placeholder = 'Select source file') {
+    return `<select data-param="${esc(name)}">${atlasSourceOptionsHtml(value || '')}</select>`;
+  };
+}
+
+if (typeof fieldInput === 'function') {
+  fieldInput = function atlasSelectedFieldInput(name, value, placeholder = 'Select field', sourceFile = '') {
+    return `<select data-param="${esc(name)}">${atlasFieldOptionsHtml(sourceFile, value || '')}</select>`;
+  };
+}
+
+function pickerSourceOptions() {
+  return atlasSelectedAuditSources().map((source) => ({
+    sourceFile: source.sourceFile,
+    sourceAlias: source.alias || source.sourceFile,
+    fieldName: '',
+    label: source.sourceFile,
+    detail: source.alias || source.sourceFile,
+    sourceColor: atlasSelectedSourceColor(source.sourceFile),
+  }));
+}
+
+function pickerFieldOptions(sourceFile = '') {
+  const sources = sourceFile ? atlasSelectedAuditSources().filter((source) => source.sourceFile === sourceFile) : atlasSelectedAuditSources();
+  return sources.flatMap((source) => (source.fields || []).map((field) => {
+    const raw = field.fieldName || field.name || field.label || '';
+    const label = field.vanityLabel || field.label || raw;
+    return {
+      sourceFile: source.sourceFile,
+      sourceAlias: source.alias || source.sourceFile,
+      fieldName: raw,
+      label,
+      detail: raw,
+      sourceColor: atlasSelectedSourceColor(source.sourceFile),
+    };
+  }));
+}
+
+function atlasSortedPickerItems(items) {
+  const list = [...items];
+  if (atlasFieldPickerSortMode === 'alpha') {
+    return list.sort((a, b) => `${a.label} ${a.fieldName}`.localeCompare(`${b.label} ${b.fieldName}`, undefined, { sensitivity: 'base', numeric: true }));
+  }
+  return list.sort((a, b) => `${a.sourceAlias} ${a.label} ${a.fieldName}`.localeCompare(`${b.sourceAlias} ${b.label} ${b.fieldName}`, undefined, { sensitivity: 'base', numeric: true }));
+}
+
+function renderMiniPicker() {
+  document.querySelector('.visual-picker-modal-backdrop.dynamic-mini-picker')?.remove();
+  if (!visualPickerState) return;
+  const options = visualPickerState.kind === 'source' ? pickerSourceOptions() : pickerFieldOptions(visualPickerState.sourceFile);
+  const query = visualPickerState.query || '';
+  const filtered = atlasSortedPickerItems(options.filter((option) => `${option.sourceFile} ${option.sourceAlias} ${option.fieldName} ${option.label}`.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase())));
+  const groups = new Map();
+  filtered.forEach((option) => {
+    const group = atlasFieldPickerSortMode === 'alpha' ? firstLetter(option.label || option.fieldName || option.sourceFile) : (visualPickerState.kind === 'source' ? 'Selected Sources' : option.sourceFile);
+    if (!groups.has(group)) groups.set(group, []);
+    groups.get(group).push(option);
+  });
+  const root = document.createElement('div');
+  root.className = 'visual-picker-modal-backdrop dynamic-mini-picker';
+  root.innerHTML = `<div class="visual-picker-modal"><div class="visual-picker-modal-header"><div><h2 class="visual-picker-modal-title">${visualPickerState.kind === 'source' ? 'Choose Selected Source File' : 'Choose Field'}</h2><p class="visual-picker-modal-subtitle">Only sources selected for this audit are available here.</p></div><button type="button" class="visual-picker-close-button" data-close-mini-picker>×</button></div><div class="visual-picker-search-row"><input data-mini-picker-search value="${esc(query)}" placeholder="Search selected sources and fields..."><div class="mini-picker-sort-toggle"><button type="button" class="secondary small ${atlasFieldPickerSortMode === 'source' ? 'active' : ''}" data-picker-sort="source">Sort by Source</button><button type="button" class="secondary small ${atlasFieldPickerSortMode === 'alpha' ? 'active' : ''}" data-picker-sort="alpha">A-Z</button></div></div><div class="visual-picker-content">${[...groups.entries()].map(([group, items]) => `<section class="visual-picker-group"><div class="visual-picker-group-header"><strong>${esc(String(group))}</strong><span>${items.length}</span></div><div class="visual-picker-pill-grid">${items.map((item) => `<button type="button" class="visual-picker-pill" data-mini-picker-value="${esc(visualPickerState.kind === 'source' ? item.sourceFile : item.fieldName)}" data-mini-picker-source="${esc(item.sourceFile)}"><span class="source-color-dot" style="background:${esc(item.sourceColor || atlasSelectedSourceColor(item.sourceFile))}"></span><span class="visual-picker-pill-main">${esc(visualPickerState.kind === 'source' ? item.sourceFile : item.label)}</span><span class="visual-picker-pill-detail">${esc(visualPickerState.kind === 'source' ? item.sourceAlias : `${item.sourceAlias}.${item.fieldName}`)}</span></button>`).join('')}</div></section>`).join('') || '<p class="hint visual-picker-empty">No selected source fields match the current search.</p>'}</div></div>`;
+  document.body.append(root);
+  root.querySelector('[data-mini-picker-search]')?.focus();
+}
+
+function atlasSourceMatchForValue(value) {
+  const normalized = String(value || '').trim();
+  if (!normalized) return null;
+  const selectedSources = state.audit.sources || [];
+  const direct = selectedSources.find((source) => source.name === normalized || (source.alias || atlasSelectedSourceAlias(source.name)) === normalized);
+  if (direct) return { sourceFile: direct.name, color: atlasSelectedSourceColor(direct.name), kind: 'source' };
+  for (const source of atlasSelectedAuditSources()) {
+    const field = (source.fields || []).find((item) => {
+      const raw = item.fieldName || item.name || item.label || '';
+      const vanity = item.vanityLabel || item.label || raw;
+      return raw === normalized || vanity === normalized || `${source.sourceFile}.${raw}` === normalized || `${source.alias || source.sourceFile}.${raw}` === normalized;
+    });
+    if (field) return { sourceFile: source.sourceFile, color: atlasSelectedSourceColor(source.sourceFile), kind: 'field' };
+  }
+  return null;
+}
+
+renderLogicWithPills = function atlasRenderLogicWithSourceAndFieldPills(text) {
+  const parts = String(text || '').split(/("[^"]+")/g);
+  return parts.map((part) => {
+    if (!part.startsWith('"') || !part.endsWith('"')) return esc(part);
+    const value = part.slice(1, -1);
+    const match = atlasSourceMatchForValue(value);
+    const klass = match?.kind === 'field' ? 'logic-pill field-pill-token' : 'logic-pill source-pill';
+    const style = match ? ` style="--pill-bg:${esc(match.color)};--pill-color:${esc(match.color)}"` : '';
+    const dot = match ? `<span class="source-color-dot" style="background:${esc(match.color)}"></span>` : '';
+    return `<span class="${klass}"${style}>${dot}${esc(value)}</span>`;
+  }).join('');
+};
+
+function atlasActionAvailability(step) {
+  const generated = !!step.autoGeneratedReference;
+  return {
+    indent: !generated,
+    outdent: !generated && !!step.parentId,
+    addChild: !generated,
+    addAfter: !generated,
+    duplicate: !generated,
+    delete: !generated,
+  };
+}
+
+function atlasAddStepAfter(stepId) {
+  capture();
+  const index = state.audit.steps.findIndex((step) => step.id === stepId);
+  const current = state.audit.steps[index];
+  const nextStep = {
+    id: uid('step'),
+    enabled: true,
+    parentId: current?.parentId || null,
+    branchType: current?.parentId ? (current.branchType || 'then') : 'root',
+    sortOrder: (Number(current?.sortOrder) || index + 1) + 0.1,
+    title: 'IF / THEN',
+    type: 'if',
+    step_type: 'if_then',
+    source: current?.source || state.audit.sources?.[0]?.name || '',
+    field: '',
+    action: '',
+    notes: '',
+    conditions: [{ id: uid('cond'), field: '', operator: 'is equal to', value: '' }],
+    params: {},
+  };
+  if (index >= 0) state.audit.steps.splice(index + 1, 0, nextStep);
+  else state.audit.steps.push(nextStep);
+  state.audit.steps.forEach((step, order) => { if (!step.autoGeneratedReference) step.sortOrder = order + 1; });
+  state.selectedStepId = nextStep.id;
+  renderAll();
+  scheduleAutosave();
+}
+
+function atlasDuplicateStepById(stepId) {
+  capture();
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === stepId);
+  if (!step || step.autoGeneratedReference) return;
+  const copy = structuredClone(step);
+  copy.id = uid('step');
+  copy.autoGeneratedReference = false;
+  copy.conditions = (copy.conditions || []).map((condition) => ({ ...condition, id: uid('cond') }));
+  const index = state.audit.steps.indexOf(step);
+  state.audit.steps.splice(index + 1, 0, copy);
+  state.audit.steps.forEach((item, order) => { if (!item.autoGeneratedReference) item.sortOrder = order + 1; });
+  state.selectedStepId = copy.id;
+  renderAll();
+  scheduleAutosave();
+}
+
+function renderLogicRuleCard(entry) {
+  const step = entry.step;
+  const selected = state.selectedStepId === step.id;
+  const generated = !!step.autoGeneratedReference;
+  const availability = atlasActionAvailability(step);
+  const cardClass = `logic-rule-card compact-logic-rule-card step-card ${generated ? 'generated-reference-step' : 'visual-step-card'} ${selected ? 'selected' : ''}`;
+  const draggable = generated ? '' : ' draggable="true"';
+  const statusLabel = generated ? 'Generated' : 'Editable';
+  const statusClass = generated ? 'blue' : 'green';
+  return `<div class="${cardClass}" data-id="${esc(step.id)}" data-parent-id="${esc(step.parentId || '')}" style="--depth:${entry.level}"${draggable}>
+    <div class="compact-rule-grid">
+      <span class="drag-handle compact-dragger" title="Drag to reorder">⋮⋮</span>
+      <span class="step-number compact-step-number">${esc(entry.number)}</span>
+      <div class="compact-rule-logic" data-select-step>${atlasRenderStepLogicWithPills(step)}</div>
+      <span class="badge ${statusClass} compact-status-badge">${statusLabel}</span>
+      <details class="compact-action-menu"><summary title="Step actions" aria-label="Step actions">⋯</summary><div class="compact-action-menu-panel">
+        <button type="button" data-select-step>Edit</button>
+        ${availability.indent ? '<button type="button" data-indent>Indent</button>' : ''}
+        ${availability.outdent ? '<button type="button" data-outdent>Outdent</button>' : ''}
+        ${availability.addChild ? '<button type="button" data-add-child-step>Add Child</button>' : ''}
+        ${availability.addAfter ? '<button type="button" data-add-step-after>Add Step After</button>' : ''}
+        ${availability.duplicate ? '<button type="button" data-duplicate-logic-step>Duplicate</button>' : ''}
+        ${availability.delete ? '<button type="button" class="danger-menu-action" data-delete-step>Delete</button>' : ''}
+      </div></details>
+    </div>
+    ${atlasRenderHiddenPreserveFields(step)}
+  </div>`;
+}
+
+function atlasRenderFieldSortControl() {
+  return `<div class="field-picker-sort-row"><span class="small">Field picker sort</span><button type="button" class="secondary small ${atlasFieldPickerSortMode === 'source' ? 'active' : ''}" data-picker-sort="source">By Source</button><button type="button" class="secondary small ${atlasFieldPickerSortMode === 'alpha' ? 'active' : ''}" data-picker-sort="alpha">A-Z</button></div>`;
+}
+
+const atlasPreviousRenderSelectedStep = renderSelectedStep;
+renderSelectedStep = function renderSelectedStepEditorPaneCompact() {
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === state.selectedStepId);
+  const target = e.selectedStepDetails || $('selectedStepDetails');
+  if (!target) return;
+  if (!step) {
+    target.innerHTML = '<p class="hint">Select or create a rule to edit step criteria.</p>';
+    setText?.('selectedRuleBadge', 'No rule selected');
+    return;
+  }
+  const number = stepNumberFor(step.id) || '?';
+  setText?.('selectedRuleBadge', `Step ${number}`);
+  target.innerHTML = `<div class="logic-editor-header"><div class="logic-editor-header-main"><span class="badge blue">Step ${esc(number)}</span><strong>${esc(step.title || atlasStepTypeLabel(step))}</strong><span class="small">${esc(atlasStepTypeLabel(step))} · ${step.autoGeneratedReference ? 'Generated reference step' : 'Editable rule'}</span></div></div><div class="callout logic-editor-generated"><strong>Live Standardised Output</strong><div class="logic-output-text" data-live-generated-output>${atlasRenderStepLogicWithPills(step)}</div></div>${atlasRenderFieldSortControl()}${atlasRenderStepEditor(step)}`;
+};
+
+const atlasPreviousRenderGuidedBuilder = renderGuidedBuilder;
+renderGuidedBuilder = function renderGuidedBuilderSelectedSourcesOnly(step) {
+  normalizeCatalogStep(step);
+  const html = atlasPreviousRenderGuidedBuilder(step);
+  return `<div class="selected-source-only-builder">${html}</div>`;
+};
+
+document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
+  const sort = event.target.closest('[data-picker-sort]');
+  if (sort) {
+    atlasFieldPickerSortMode = sort.dataset.pickerSort || 'source';
+    if (visualPickerState) renderMiniPicker();
+    else renderSelectedStep();
+    return;
+  }
+  const after = event.target.closest('[data-add-step-after]');
+  if (after) {
+    const card = after.closest('.step-card');
+    if (card) atlasAddStepAfter(card.dataset.id);
+    return;
+  }
+  const duplicate = event.target.closest('[data-duplicate-logic-step]');
+  if (duplicate) {
+    const card = duplicate.closest('.step-card');
+    if (card) atlasDuplicateStepById(card.dataset.id);
+    return;
+  }
+}, true);
+
+if (typeof sourceOptionsHtml === 'function') {
+  sourceOptionsHtml = function atlasSelectedSourceOptionsHtmlForGuidedBuilders(selected = '') {
+    return atlasSourceOptionsHtml(selected || '');
+  };
+}
+
+if (typeof fieldOptionsHtml === 'function') {
+  fieldOptionsHtml = function atlasSelectedFieldOptionsHtmlForGuidedBuilders(sourceFile = '', selected = '') {
+    return atlasFieldOptionsHtml(sourceFile || '', selected || '');
+  };
+}
+
+/* JOIN Venn Diagram Picker */
+let atlasJoinPickerState = null;
+
+function atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(joinType) {
+  const normalized = String(joinType || 'INNER JOIN').toUpperCase();
+  if (normalized === 'LEFT JOIN') return 'LEFT JOIN';
+  if (normalized === 'RIGHT JOIN') return 'RIGHT JOIN';
+  if (normalized === 'FULL OUTER JOIN') return 'FULL OUTER JOIN';
+  return 'INNER JOIN';
+}
+
+function atlasJoinKeptRowsDescription(joinType) {
+  const normalized = atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(joinType);
+  if (normalized === 'LEFT JOIN') return 'All rows from the left source, plus matching rows from the right source.';
+  if (normalized === 'RIGHT JOIN') return 'All rows from the right source, plus matching rows from the left source.';
+  if (normalized === 'FULL OUTER JOIN') return 'All rows from both sources, whether matched or unmatched.';
+  return 'Only rows where the left and right sources match.';
+}
+
+function atlasJoinSourceLabel(sourceFile) {
+  if (!sourceFile) return '[source not selected]';
+  const alias = sourceAliasFor?.(sourceFile) || sourceFile;
+  return alias && alias !== sourceFile ? `${alias} (${sourceFile})` : sourceFile;
+}
+
+function atlasJoinDeveloperLogic(step) {
+  normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+  const p = step.params || {};
+  const joinType = atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(p.join_type);
+  const leftSource = p.left_source || '[left source]';
+  const rightSource = p.right_source || '[right source]';
+  const leftAlias = sourceAliasFor?.(leftSource) || leftSource;
+  const rightAlias = sourceAliasFor?.(rightSource) || rightSource;
+  const leftField = p.left_field || '[left key]';
+  const rightField = p.right_field || '[right key]';
+  return `${joinType}: ${atlasJoinKeptRowsDescription(joinType)} Join ${leftAlias}.${leftField} to ${rightAlias}.${rightField}.`;
+}
+
+function atlasRenderJoinTypePickerButton(step) {
+  const joinType = atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(step.params?.join_type);
+  return `<div class="join-type-trigger"><span>Join Type</span><input type="hidden" data-param="join_type" value="${esc(joinType)}"><div class="join-type-trigger-row"><span class="join-type-value-pill">${esc(joinType)}</span><button type="button" class="btn secondary small" data-open-join-picker="${esc(step.id)}">Choose Join Type</button></div><p class="hint">${esc(atlasJoinKeptRowsDescription(joinType))}</p></div>`;
+}
+
+const atlasJoinPreviousRenderGuidedBuilder = renderGuidedBuilder;
+renderGuidedBuilder = function renderGuidedBuilderWithJoinVenn(step) {
+  normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+  if (step.step_type !== 'join') return atlasJoinPreviousRenderGuidedBuilder(step);
+  const p = step.params || {};
+  return `<div class="guided-builder join-guided-builder"><h4>Join</h4>${atlasRenderJoinTypePickerButton(step)}<label>Left Source${sourceInput('left_source', p.left_source)}</label><label>Left Field${fieldInput('left_field', p.left_field, 'Select field', p.left_source)}</label><label>Right Source${sourceInput('right_source', p.right_source)}</label><label>Right Field${fieldInput('right_field', p.right_field, 'Select field', p.right_source)}</label></div>`;
+};
+
+function atlasOpenJoinPicker(stepId) {
+  const root = document.querySelector('[data-live-editor-step-id]');
+  if (root) atlasCommitLiveEditor?.({ rerender: false });
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === stepId || item.id === state.selectedStepId);
+  if (!step || step.step_type !== 'join') return;
+  normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+  atlasJoinPickerState = { stepId: step.id, pendingJoinType: atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(step.params?.join_type) };
+  atlasRenderJoinPickerModal();
+}
+
+function atlasRenderJoinPickerModal() {
+  document.querySelector('.join-picker-backdrop')?.remove();
+  if (!atlasJoinPickerState) return;
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === atlasJoinPickerState.stepId);
+  if (!step) return;
+  const p = step.params || {};
+  const joinType = atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(atlasJoinPickerState.pendingJoinType);
+  const leftColor = atlasSelectedSourceColor?.(p.left_source) || SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE?.[0] || '#4d6fa9';
+  const rightColor = atlasSelectedSourceColor?.(p.right_source) || SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE?.[1] || '#b54b4b';
+  const leftLabel = atlasJoinSourceLabel(p.left_source || 'Left Source');
+  const rightLabel = atlasJoinSourceLabel(p.right_source || 'Right Source');
+  const root = document.createElement('div');
+  root.className = 'join-picker-backdrop';
+  root.innerHTML = `<div class="join-picker-modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true"><div class="join-picker-header"><div><h2 class="join-picker-title">Choose Join Type</h2><p class="hint">Preview which data will be kept before confirming the JOIN type.</p></div><button type="button" class="btn ghost small" data-close-join-picker>Cancel</button></div><div class="join-picker-body"><section class="join-picker-stage"><div class="join-venn-wrap" data-join-type="${esc(joinType)}" style="--join-left-color:${esc(leftColor)};--join-right-color:${esc(rightColor)}"><div class="join-venn-circle join-venn-left"></div><div class="join-venn-circle join-venn-right"></div><div class="join-venn-intersection"></div><div class="join-venn-label left">${esc(leftLabel)}</div><div class="join-venn-label right">${esc(rightLabel)}</div></div><div class="join-picker-option-grid">${['INNER JOIN','LEFT JOIN','RIGHT JOIN','FULL OUTER JOIN'].map((type) => `<button type="button" class="join-picker-option ${type === joinType ? 'active' : ''}" data-join-type-option="${esc(type)}">${esc(type)}</button>`).join('')}</div></section><section class="join-picker-details"><div class="join-picker-summary-card"><strong>${esc(joinType)}</strong><p>${esc(atlasJoinKeptRowsDescription(joinType))}</p></div><div class="join-picker-table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Join Input</th><th>Dynamic Label</th><th>Join Key</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Left Source</td><td>${esc(leftLabel)}</td><td>${esc(p.left_field || '[left key not selected]')}</td></tr><tr><td>Right Source</td><td>${esc(rightLabel)}</td><td>${esc(p.right_field || '[right key not selected]')}</td></tr><tr><td>Developer Logic</td><td colspan="2">${esc(atlasJoinDeveloperLogic({ ...step, params: { ...p, join_type: joinType } }))}</td></tr></tbody></table></div></section></div><div class="join-picker-footer"><p class="hint">Confirm is required to preserve the selected JOIN type.</p><div class="toolbar compact-toolbar"><button type="button" class="btn ghost" data-close-join-picker>Cancel</button><button type="button" class="btn primary" data-confirm-join-picker>Confirm</button></div></div></div>`;
+  document.body.append(root);
+}
+
+function atlasConfirmJoinPicker() {
+  if (!atlasJoinPickerState) return;
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === atlasJoinPickerState.stepId);
+  if (!step) return;
+  step.params = step.params || {};
+  step.params.join_type = atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(atlasJoinPickerState.pendingJoinType);
+  const editor = document.querySelector(`[data-live-editor-step-id="${CSS.escape(step.id)}"]`);
+  const hidden = editor?.querySelector('[data-param="join_type"]');
+  if (hidden) hidden.value = step.params.join_type;
+  atlasJoinPickerState = null;
+  document.querySelector('.join-picker-backdrop')?.remove();
+  renderAll();
+  scheduleAutosave();
+}
+
+document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
+  const openButton = event.target.closest('[data-open-join-picker]');
+  if (openButton) {
+    atlasOpenJoinPicker(openButton.dataset.openJoinPicker || state.selectedStepId);
+    return;
+  }
+  const joinOption = event.target.closest('[data-join-type-option]');
+  if (joinOption && atlasJoinPickerState) {
+    atlasJoinPickerState.pendingJoinType = joinOption.dataset.joinTypeOption;
+    atlasRenderJoinPickerModal();
+    return;
+  }
+  if (event.target.closest('[data-confirm-join-picker]')) {
+    atlasConfirmJoinPicker();
+    return;
+  }
+  if (event.target.closest('[data-close-join-picker]')) {
+    atlasJoinPickerState = null;
+    document.querySelector('.join-picker-backdrop')?.remove();
+  }
+}, true);
+
+const atlasJoinPreviousRenderLogicTablePreviewRows = typeof renderLogicTablePreviewRows === 'function' ? renderLogicTablePreviewRows : null;
+renderLogicTablePreviewRows = function renderLogicTablePreviewRowsWithDeveloperLogic() {
+  const target = $('logicBuilderTableBody');
+  if (!target) return;
+  const entries = atlasVisibleStepEntries ? atlasVisibleStepEntries() : hierarchicalStepEntries({ includeDisabled: true });
+  target.innerHTML = entries.length ? entries.map((entry) => {
+    const dev = typeof developerLogic === 'function' ? developerLogic(entry.step) : '';
+    return `<tr class="${state.selectedStepId === entry.step.id ? 'selected-row' : ''}" data-table-step-id="${esc(entry.step.id)}"><td>${esc(entry.number)}</td><td>${esc(atlasStepTypeLabel ? atlasStepTypeLabel(entry.step) : (entry.step.step_type || 'Logic Step'))}</td><td>${atlasRenderStepLogicWithPills(entry.step)}</td><td>${esc(dev)}</td><td>${entry.step.enabled === false ? '<span class="badge red">Disabled</span>' : '<span class="badge blue">Active</span>'}</td></tr>`;
+  }).join('') : '<tr><td colspan="5" class="hint">No logic steps yet.</td></tr>';
+};
+
+/* JOIN Venn overlap refinement - accurate two-circle overlap shading */
+function atlasJoinIncludedRegionMarkup(joinType, clipId) {
+  const normalized = atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(joinType);
+  if (normalized === 'INNER JOIN') {
+    return `<circle class="join-venn-included join-venn-included-intersection" cx="155" cy="142" r="112" clip-path="url(#${clipId}-right)"></circle>`;
+  }
+  if (normalized === 'LEFT JOIN') {
+    return `<circle class="join-venn-included join-venn-included-left" cx="155" cy="142" r="112"></circle>`;
+  }
+  if (normalized === 'RIGHT JOIN') {
+    return `<circle class="join-venn-included join-venn-included-right" cx="245" cy="142" r="112"></circle>`;
+  }
+  return `<circle class="join-venn-included join-venn-included-left" cx="155" cy="142" r="112"></circle><circle class="join-venn-included join-venn-included-right" cx="245" cy="142" r="112"></circle>`;
+}
+
+function atlasJoinVennSvg(joinType, leftLabel, rightLabel, leftColor, rightColor) {
+  const normalized = atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(joinType);
+  const clipId = `joinClip${String(normalized).replace(/[^A-Z]/g, '')}`;
+  return `<svg class="join-venn-svg" viewBox="0 0 400 300" role="img" aria-label="${esc(normalized)} Venn diagram showing included join output rows">
+    <defs>
+      <clipPath id="${clipId}-left"><circle cx="155" cy="142" r="112"></circle></clipPath>
+      <clipPath id="${clipId}-right"><circle cx="245" cy="142" r="112"></circle></clipPath>
+    </defs>
+    <rect class="join-venn-canvas" x="0" y="0" width="400" height="300" rx="18"></rect>
+    <circle class="join-venn-base join-venn-base-left" cx="155" cy="142" r="112" style="--join-source-color:${esc(leftColor)}"></circle>
+    <circle class="join-venn-base join-venn-base-right" cx="245" cy="142" r="112" style="--join-source-color:${esc(rightColor)}"></circle>
+    <circle class="join-venn-overlap-outline" cx="155" cy="142" r="112" clip-path="url(#${clipId}-right)"></circle>
+    ${atlasJoinIncludedRegionMarkup(normalized, clipId)}
+    <circle class="join-venn-stroke" cx="155" cy="142" r="112" style="--join-source-color:${esc(leftColor)}"></circle>
+    <circle class="join-venn-stroke" cx="245" cy="142" r="112" style="--join-source-color:${esc(rightColor)}"></circle>
+    <text class="join-venn-area-label join-venn-left-only-label" x="106" y="145">Left only</text>
+    <text class="join-venn-area-label join-venn-match-label" x="200" y="145">Matched</text>
+    <text class="join-venn-area-label join-venn-right-only-label" x="294" y="145">Right only</text>
+    <foreignObject x="20" y="246" width="166" height="42"><div class="join-venn-source-label join-venn-source-left">${esc(leftLabel)}</div></foreignObject>
+    <foreignObject x="214" y="246" width="166" height="42"><div class="join-venn-source-label join-venn-source-right">${esc(rightLabel)}</div></foreignObject>
+  </svg>`;
+}
+
+atlasRenderJoinPickerModal = function atlasRenderJoinPickerModalOverlapRefinement() {
+  document.querySelector('.join-picker-backdrop')?.remove();
+  if (!atlasJoinPickerState) return;
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === atlasJoinPickerState.stepId);
+  if (!step) return;
+  const p = step.params || {};
+  const joinType = atlasJoinTypeDisplayName(atlasJoinPickerState.pendingJoinType);
+  const leftColor = atlasSelectedSourceColor?.(p.left_source) || SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE?.[0] || '#4d6fa9';
+  const rightColor = atlasSelectedSourceColor?.(p.right_source) || SOURCE_COLOR_PALETTE?.[1] || '#b54b4b';
+  const leftLabel = atlasJoinSourceLabel(p.left_source || 'Left Source');
+  const rightLabel = atlasJoinSourceLabel(p.right_source || 'Right Source');
+  const root = document.createElement('div');
+  root.className = 'join-picker-backdrop';
+  root.innerHTML = `<div class="join-picker-modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true"><div class="join-picker-header"><div><h2 class="join-picker-title">Choose Join Type</h2><p class="hint">The diagram always shows the left-only, matched, and right-only regions. Dark shading indicates what will be kept.</p></div><button type="button" class="btn ghost small" data-close-join-picker>Cancel</button></div><div class="join-picker-body"><section class="join-picker-stage"><div class="join-venn-wrap join-venn-svg-wrap" data-join-type="${esc(joinType)}" style="--join-left-color:${esc(leftColor)};--join-right-color:${esc(rightColor)}">${atlasJoinVennSvg(joinType, leftLabel, rightLabel, leftColor, rightColor)}</div><div class="join-picker-option-grid">${['INNER JOIN','LEFT JOIN','RIGHT JOIN','FULL OUTER JOIN'].map((type) => `<button type="button" class="join-picker-option ${type === joinType ? 'active' : ''}" data-join-type-option="${esc(type)}">${esc(type)}</button>`).join('')}</div></section><section class="join-picker-details"><div class="join-picker-summary-card"><strong>${esc(joinType)}</strong><p>${esc(atlasJoinKeptRowsDescription(joinType))}</p></div><div class="join-picker-table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>Join Input</th><th>Dynamic Label</th><th>Join Key</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>Left Source</td><td>${esc(leftLabel)}</td><td>${esc(p.left_field || '[left key not selected]')}</td></tr><tr><td>Right Source</td><td>${esc(rightLabel)}</td><td>${esc(p.right_field || '[right key not selected]')}</td></tr><tr><td>Developer Logic</td><td colspan="2">${esc(atlasJoinDeveloperLogic({ ...step, params: { ...p, join_type: joinType } }))}</td></tr></tbody></table></div></section></div><div class="join-picker-footer"><p class="hint">Confirm is required to preserve the selected JOIN type.</p><div class="toolbar compact-toolbar"><button type="button" class="btn ghost" data-close-join-picker>Cancel</button><button type="button" class="btn primary" data-confirm-join-picker>Confirm</button></div></div></div>`;
+  document.body.append(root);
+};
+
+
+/* Logic Step Editor usability refinement */
+let atlasIfThenEditorTab = 'conditions';
+
+function atlasConditionValues(value) {
+  return String(value || '')
+    .split(/\r?\n|,/)
+    .map((item) => item.trim())
+    .filter(Boolean);
+}
+
+function atlasConditionValueRows(value) {
+  const values = atlasConditionValues(value);
+  const list = values.length ? values : [''];
+  return list.map((item) => `<div class="condition-value-row"><input data-condition-value-item value="${esc(item)}" placeholder="Comparison value"><button type="button" class="secondary small" data-remove-condition-value>Remove</button></div>`).join('');
+}
+
+function atlasUpdateConditionValueHidden(container) {
+  if (!container) return;
+  const hidden = container.querySelector('[data-condition-field="compareValue"]');
+  if (!hidden) return;
+  hidden.value = [...container.querySelectorAll('[data-condition-value-item]')]
+    .map((input) => String(input.value || '').trim())
+    .filter(Boolean)
+    .join('\n');
+}
+
+renderConditionRow = function renderConditionRowUsability(condition, index) {
+  const sourceFile = condition.sourceFile || '';
+  const valueMode = condition.valueMode || 'values';
+  return `<div class="guided-condition-row refined-condition-row" data-condition-index="${index}" data-condition-id="${esc(condition.id || uid('cond'))}">
+    <div class="condition-row-header"><span class="badge blue">${index === 0 ? 'IF' : esc(condition.connector || 'AND')}</span><select data-condition-field="connector"><option ${index === 0 ? 'selected' : ''}>IF</option><option ${condition.connector === 'AND' ? 'selected' : ''}>AND</option><option ${condition.connector === 'OR' ? 'selected' : ''}>OR</option></select></div>
+    <label>Source File${renderConditionPickerTrigger('source', 'sourceFile', sourceFile, sourceFile || 'Choose source')}</label>
+    <label>Field${renderConditionPickerTrigger('field', 'fieldName', condition.fieldName || '', condition.fieldName || 'Choose field', sourceFile)}</label>
+    <label>Operator<select data-condition-field="operator">${optionsHtml(catalogArray('app_config.logic_options.operators', ['is equal to']), condition.operator)}</select></label>
+    <label>Value Mode<select data-condition-field="valueMode"><option value="values" ${valueMode === 'values' ? 'selected' : ''}>Values</option><option value="field" ${valueMode === 'field' ? 'selected' : ''}>Compare to Field</option><option value="blank" ${valueMode === 'blank' ? 'selected' : ''}>Blank / Null</option><option value="expression" ${valueMode === 'expression' ? 'selected' : ''}>Expression</option></select></label>
+    ${valueMode === 'field'
+      ? `<label>Compare Source${renderConditionPickerTrigger('source', 'compareSourceFile', condition.compareSourceFile || sourceFile, condition.compareSourceFile || sourceFile || 'Choose source')}</label><label>Compare Field${renderConditionPickerTrigger('field', 'compareFieldName', condition.compareFieldName || '', condition.compareFieldName || 'Choose field', condition.compareSourceFile || sourceFile)}</label>`
+      : valueMode === 'blank'
+        ? `<div class="logic-editor-callout full">No comparison value required.</div>`
+        : valueMode === 'expression'
+          ? `<label class="full">Expression<textarea data-condition-field="expression">${esc(condition.expression || '')}</textarea></label>`
+          : `<div class="condition-values-editor full"><div class="title-row compact-row"><label>Comparison Values</label><button type="button" class="secondary small" data-add-condition-value>Add Value</button></div><input type="hidden" data-condition-field="compareValue" value="${esc(valuesToLines(condition.compareValue || ''))}"><div class="condition-values-list">${atlasConditionValueRows(condition.compareValue || '')}</div></div>`}
+    <button type="button" class="danger small" data-guided-delete-condition>Delete Condition</button>
+  </div>`;
+};
+
+sourceInput = function atlasSelectedSourceVisualInput(name, value, placeholder = 'Select source file') {
+  return renderMiniPickerTrigger('source', name, value || '', value || placeholder);
+};
+
+fieldInput = function atlasSelectedFieldVisualInput(name, value, placeholder = 'Select field', sourceFile = '') {
+  return renderMiniPickerTrigger('field', name, value || '', value || placeholder, sourceFile || '');
+};
+
+function atlasRenderThenActionBuilder(p, prefix = 'THEN') {
+  return `<div class="then-action-builder refined-action-builder"><h4>${prefix} Action Builder</h4><label>${prefix} Action Type<select data-param="then_action_type"><option value="freeform" ${p.then_action_type === 'freeform' ? 'selected' : ''}>Freeform Text</option><option value="set_column_value" ${p.then_action_type === 'set_column_value' ? 'selected' : ''}>Set Column Value from Field</option><option value="display_text" ${p.then_action_type === 'display_text' ? 'selected' : ''}>Display Text Response in Column</option><option value="do_not_display" ${p.then_action_type === 'do_not_display' ? 'selected' : ''}>Do Not Display</option></select></label><label class="full">${prefix} Action<textarea data-param="then_action" placeholder="e.g., display records in the audit">${esc(p.then_action || '')}</textarea></label><label>Display Text<input data-param="then_text" value="${esc(p.then_text || '')}" placeholder="e.g., Research IT0027"></label><label>Output Column${fieldInput('then_output_column', p.then_output_column || 'Error Message')}</label><label>Target Column${fieldInput('then_target_column', p.then_target_column)}</label><label>Value Source${sourceInput('then_value_source_file', p.then_value_source_file || p.source_file)}</label><label>Value Field${fieldInput('then_value_field', p.then_value_field, 'Select field', p.then_value_source_file || p.source_file)}</label></div>`;
+}
+
+function atlasRenderIfThenTabbedBuilder(step) {
+  normalizeCatalogStep(step);
+  const p = step.params || {};
+  const conditionsHtml = `<div class="condition-guided-stack">${parseStepConditions(step).map((condition, index) => renderConditionRow(condition, index)).join('')}</div><div class="row"><button type="button" class="secondary small" data-guided-add-condition="AND">+ AND</button><button type="button" class="secondary small" data-guided-add-condition="OR">+ OR</button></div>`;
+  const actionHtml = `${atlasRenderThenActionBuilder(p)}<label><span class="check"><input type="checkbox" data-param="include_else_branch" ${p.include_else_branch === 'Yes' ? 'checked' : ''}> Include ELSE branch</span></label><button type="button" class="secondary small" data-create-else-step>Create / Update ELSE Branch</button>`;
+  return `<div class="guided-builder if-then-tabbed-builder"><div class="logic-editor-tabs"><button type="button" class="logic-editor-tab ${atlasIfThenEditorTab === 'conditions' ? 'active' : ''}" data-editor-tab="conditions">Condition Builder</button><button type="button" class="logic-editor-tab ${atlasIfThenEditorTab === 'actions' ? 'active' : ''}" data-editor-tab="actions">Action Builder</button></div><div class="logic-editor-tab-panel ${atlasIfThenEditorTab === 'conditions' ? 'active' : ''}" data-editor-panel="conditions">${conditionsHtml}</div><div class="logic-editor-tab-panel ${atlasIfThenEditorTab === 'actions' ? 'active' : ''}" data-editor-panel="actions">${actionHtml}</div></div>`;
+}
+
+const atlasUsabilityPreviousRenderGuidedBuilder = renderGuidedBuilder;
+renderGuidedBuilder = function renderGuidedBuilderUsability(step) {
+  normalizeCatalogStep(step);
+  if (step.step_type === 'if_then') return atlasRenderIfThenTabbedBuilder(step);
+  if (step.step_type === 'else') return `<div class="guided-builder"><h4>ELSE Action</h4><p class="hint">ELSE can apply an action or end with a colon so nested sub-steps can continue beneath it.</p>${atlasRenderThenActionBuilder(step.params || {}, 'ELSE')}</div>`;
+  return atlasUsabilityPreviousRenderGuidedBuilder(step);
+};
+
+atlasRenderStepEditor = function atlasRenderStepEditorUsability(step) {
+  if (!step || step.autoGeneratedReference) {
+    return '<div class="logic-editor-section"><p class="hint">Generated reference steps are controlled by selected source files. Select a manual rule to edit its logic criteria.</p></div>';
+  }
+  normalizeCatalogStep(step);
+  return `<div class="logic-editor-form" data-live-editor-step-id="${esc(step.id)}">
+    <section class="logic-editor-section step-settings-section">
+      <h3>Step Settings</h3>
+      <div class="logic-editor-grid single-column-settings">
+        <label><span>Step Type</span><select data-live-step-type-select>${atlasSelectOptions((STEP_TYPE_OPTIONS || []).filter(([value]) => value !== 'reference_source'), step.step_type)}</select></label>
+      </div>
+    </section>
+    <section class="logic-editor-section criteria-section">
+      ${renderGuidedBuilder(step)}
+    </section>
+    <section class="logic-editor-section notes-section">
+      <h3>Notes</h3>
+      <label class="full"><span>Manual Notes</span><textarea data-step-notes-live placeholder="Notes for this step">${esc(step.notes || '')}</textarea></label>
+    </section>
+  </div>`;
+};
+
+renderSelectedStep = function renderSelectedStepUsabilityPane() {
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === state.selectedStepId);
+  const target = e.selectedStepDetails || $('selectedStepDetails');
+  if (!target) return;
+  if (!step) {
+    target.innerHTML = '<p class="hint">Select or create a rule to edit step criteria.</p>';
+    setText?.('selectedRuleBadge', 'No rule selected');
+    return;
+  }
+  const number = stepNumberFor(step.id) || '?';
+  setText?.('selectedRuleBadge', `Step ${number}`);
+  target.innerHTML = `${atlasRenderFieldSortControl?.() || ''}${atlasRenderStepEditor(step)}`;
+};
+
+document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
+  const editorTab = event.target.closest('[data-editor-tab]');
+  if (editorTab) {
+    atlasIfThenEditorTab = editorTab.dataset.editorTab || 'conditions';
+    renderSelectedStep();
+    return;
+  }
+  const addValue = event.target.closest('[data-add-condition-value]');
+  if (addValue) {
+    const editor = addValue.closest('.condition-values-editor');
+    const list = editor?.querySelector('.condition-values-list');
+    if (list) list.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', atlasConditionValueRows(''));
+    atlasUpdateConditionValueHidden(editor);
+    return;
+  }
+  const removeValue = event.target.closest('[data-remove-condition-value]');
+  if (removeValue) {
+    const editor = removeValue.closest('.condition-values-editor');
+    const row = removeValue.closest('.condition-value-row');
+    row?.remove();
+    if (editor && !editor.querySelector('[data-condition-value-item]')) {
+      editor.querySelector('.condition-values-list')?.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', atlasConditionValueRows(''));
+    }
+    atlasUpdateConditionValueHidden(editor);
+    atlasCommitLiveEditor?.({ rerender: false });
+  }
+}, true);
+
+document.addEventListener('input', (event) => {
+  if (!event.target.matches('[data-condition-value-item]')) return;
+  const editor = event.target.closest('.condition-values-editor');
+  atlasUpdateConditionValueHidden(editor);
+}, true);
+
+
+/* Follow-up Logic Step Editor refinements */
+function atlasOperatorOptionsForValueMode(valueMode, selectedOperator = '') {
+  const mode = valueMode || 'values';
+  const operators = mode === 'blank'
+    ? ['is blank/null', 'is not blank/null']
+    : mode === 'field'
+      ? ['is equal to', 'is not equal to', 'is greater than', 'is greater than or equal to', 'is less than', 'is less than or equal to']
+      : mode === 'expression'
+        ? ['matches expression', 'does not match expression']
+        : ['is equal to', 'is not equal to', 'contains', 'does not contain', 'starts with', 'ends with'];
+  return optionsHtml(operators, operators.includes(selectedOperator) ? selectedOperator : operators[0]);
+}
+
+renderConditionRow = function renderConditionRowFollowUp(condition, index) {
+  const sourceFile = condition.sourceFile || '';
+  const valueMode = condition.valueMode || 'values';
+  return `<div class="guided-condition-row refined-condition-row" data-condition-index="${index}" data-condition-id="${esc(condition.id || uid('cond'))}">
+    <div class="condition-row-header"><span class="badge blue">${index === 0 ? 'IF' : esc(condition.connector || 'AND')}</span><select data-condition-field="connector"><option ${index === 0 ? 'selected' : ''}>IF</option><option ${condition.connector === 'AND' ? 'selected' : ''}>AND</option><option ${condition.connector === 'OR' ? 'selected' : ''}>OR</option></select></div>
+    <label>Source File${renderConditionPickerTrigger('source', 'sourceFile', sourceFile, sourceFile || 'Choose source')}</label>
+    <label>Field${renderConditionPickerTrigger('field', 'fieldName', condition.fieldName || '', condition.fieldName || 'Choose field', sourceFile)}</label>
+    <label>Value Mode<select data-condition-field="valueMode"><option value="values" ${valueMode === 'values' ? 'selected' : ''}>Values</option><option value="field" ${valueMode === 'field' ? 'selected' : ''}>Compare to Field</option><option value="blank" ${valueMode === 'blank' ? 'selected' : ''}>Blank / Null</option><option value="expression" ${valueMode === 'expression' ? 'selected' : ''}>Expression</option></select></label>
+    <label>Operator<select data-condition-field="operator">${atlasOperatorOptionsForValueMode(valueMode, condition.operator)}</select></label>
+    ${valueMode === 'field'
+      ? `<label>Compare Source${renderConditionPickerTrigger('source', 'compareSourceFile', condition.compareSourceFile || sourceFile, condition.compareSourceFile || sourceFile || 'Choose source')}</label><label>Compare Field${renderConditionPickerTrigger('field', 'compareFieldName', condition.compareFieldName || '', condition.compareFieldName || 'Choose field', condition.compareSourceFile || sourceFile)}</label>`
+      : valueMode === 'blank'
+        ? `<div class="logic-editor-callout full">No comparison value required.</div>`
+        : valueMode === 'expression'
+          ? `<label class="full">Expression<textarea data-condition-field="expression">${esc(condition.expression || '')}</textarea></label>`
+          : `<div class="condition-values-editor full"><div class="title-row compact-row"><label>Comparison Values</label><button type="button" class="secondary small" data-add-condition-value>Add Value</button></div><input type="hidden" data-condition-field="compareValue" value="${esc(valuesToLines(condition.compareValue || ''))}"><div class="condition-values-list">${atlasConditionValueRows(condition.compareValue || '')}</div></div>`}
+    <button type="button" class="danger small" data-guided-delete-condition>Delete Condition</button>
+  </div>`;
+};
+
+function atlasRenderThenActionBuilder(p, prefix = 'THEN') {
+  const actionType = p.then_action_type || 'freeform';
+  const typeSelect = `<label class="full action-type-row"><span>${prefix} Action Type</span><select data-param="then_action_type"><option value="freeform" ${actionType === 'freeform' ? 'selected' : ''}>Freeform Text</option><option value="set_column_value" ${actionType === 'set_column_value' ? 'selected' : ''}>Set Column Value from Field</option><option value="display_text" ${actionType === 'display_text' ? 'selected' : ''}>Display Text Response in Column</option><option value="do_not_display" ${actionType === 'do_not_display' ? 'selected' : ''}>Do Not Display</option></select></label>`;
+  if (actionType === 'set_column_value') {
+    return `<div class="then-action-builder refined-action-builder dynamic-action-builder">${typeSelect}<label>Target Column${fieldInput('then_target_column', p.then_target_column, 'Select target field')}</label><label>Value Source${sourceInput('then_value_source_file', p.then_value_source_file || p.source_file)}</label><label>Value Field${fieldInput('then_value_field', p.then_value_field, 'Select value field', p.then_value_source_file || p.source_file)}</label><label class="full">Action Notes<textarea data-param="then_action" placeholder="Optional notes for this action">${esc(p.then_action || '')}</textarea></label></div>`;
+  }
+  if (actionType === 'display_text') {
+    return `<div class="then-action-builder refined-action-builder dynamic-action-builder">${typeSelect}<label class="full">Display Text<input data-param="then_text" value="${esc(p.then_text || '')}" placeholder="Text to display for matching records"></label><label>Output Column${fieldInput('then_output_column', p.then_output_column || 'Error Message')}</label><label>Reference Field${fieldInput('then_target_column', p.then_target_column, 'Optional reference field')}</label><label class="full">Action Notes<textarea data-param="then_action" placeholder="Optional notes for this action">${esc(p.then_action || '')}</textarea></label></div>`;
+  }
+  if (actionType === 'do_not_display') {
+    return `<div class="then-action-builder refined-action-builder dynamic-action-builder">${typeSelect}<div class="logic-editor-callout full">Matching records will not be displayed in the audit output for this branch.</div><input type="hidden" data-param="then_action" value="${esc(p.then_action || 'Do not display matching records')}"></div>`;
+  }
+  return `<div class="then-action-builder refined-action-builder dynamic-action-builder">${typeSelect}<label class="full">Action<textarea data-param="then_action" placeholder="e.g., display records in the audit">${esc(p.then_action || '')}</textarea></label></div>`;
+}
+
+function atlasRenderIfThenTabbedBuilder(step) {
+  normalizeCatalogStep(step);
+  const p = step.params || {};
+  const conditionsHtml = `<div class="condition-guided-stack">${parseStepConditions(step).map((condition, index) => renderConditionRow(condition, index)).join('')}</div><div class="row"><button type="button" class="secondary small" data-guided-add-condition="AND">+ AND</button><button type="button" class="secondary small" data-guided-add-condition="OR">+ OR</button></div>`;
+  const checked = p.include_else_branch === 'Yes';
+  const actionHtml = `${atlasRenderThenActionBuilder(p)}<div class="else-branch-controls"><label><span class="check"><input type="checkbox" data-param="include_else_branch" ${checked ? 'checked' : ''}> Include ELSE branch</span></label><button type="button" class="secondary small" data-create-else-step>${checked ? 'Create / Update ELSE Branch' : 'Enable ELSE Branch'}</button></div>`;
+  return `<div class="guided-builder if-then-tabbed-builder"><div class="logic-editor-tabs"><button type="button" class="logic-editor-tab ${atlasIfThenEditorTab === 'conditions' ? 'active' : ''}" data-editor-tab="conditions">Condition Builder</button><button type="button" class="logic-editor-tab ${atlasIfThenEditorTab === 'actions' ? 'active' : ''}" data-editor-tab="actions">Action Builder</button></div><div class="logic-editor-tab-panel ${atlasIfThenEditorTab === 'conditions' ? 'active' : ''}" data-editor-panel="conditions">${conditionsHtml}</div><div class="logic-editor-tab-panel ${atlasIfThenEditorTab === 'actions' ? 'active' : ''}" data-editor-panel="actions">${actionHtml}</div></div>`;
+}
+
+renderGuidedBuilder = function renderGuidedBuilderFollowUp(step) {
+  normalizeCatalogStep(step);
+  if (step.step_type === 'if_then') return atlasRenderIfThenTabbedBuilder(step);
+  if (step.step_type === 'else') return `<div class="guided-builder else-action-builder"><p class="hint">Configure what happens when the IF conditions are not met.</p>${atlasRenderThenActionBuilder(step.params || {}, 'ELSE')}</div>`;
+  return typeof atlasJoinPreviousRenderGuidedBuilder === 'function' ? atlasJoinPreviousRenderGuidedBuilder(step) : atlasUsabilityPreviousRenderGuidedBuilder(step);
+};
+
+renderSelectedStep = function renderSelectedStepFollowUpPane() {
+  const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === state.selectedStepId);
+  const target = e.selectedStepDetails || $('selectedStepDetails');
+  if (!target) return;
+  if (!step) {
+    target.innerHTML = '<p class="hint">Select or create a rule to edit step criteria.</p>';
+    setText?.('selectedRuleBadge', 'No rule selected');
+    return;
+  }
+  const number = stepNumberFor(step.id) || '?';
+  setText?.('selectedRuleBadge', `Step ${number}`);
+  target.innerHTML = `${atlasRenderStepEditor(step)}`;
+  requestAnimationFrame(atlasSyncRulesHeightToEditor);
+};
+
+function atlasSyncRulesHeightToEditor() {
+  const editor = document.querySelector('.live-output-card');
+  const rules = document.querySelector('.logic-rules-card');
+  const list = document.querySelector('.logic-rules-card .logic-list');
+  if (!editor || !rules || !list) return;
+  const height = Math.max(360, Math.ceil(editor.getBoundingClientRect().height));
+  rules.style.height = `${height}px`;
+  rules.style.minHeight = `${height}px`;
+  const header = rules.querySelector('.section-title')?.getBoundingClientRect().height || 0;
+  const hint = rules.querySelector('.hint')?.getBoundingClientRect().height || 0;
+  list.style.maxHeight = `${Math.max(220, height - header - hint - 58)}px`;
+  list.style.overflowY = 'auto';
+}
+
+if (typeof ResizeObserver !== 'undefined') {
+  const atlasLogicResizeObserver = new ResizeObserver(() => atlasSyncRulesHeightToEditor());
+  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
+    const editor = document.querySelector('.live-output-card');
+    if (editor) atlasLogicResizeObserver.observe(editor);
+    atlasSyncRulesHeightToEditor();
+  });
+} else {
+  window.addEventListener('resize', atlasSyncRulesHeightToEditor);
+}
+
+const atlasFollowUpPreviousRenderSteps = renderSteps;
+renderSteps = function renderStepsFollowUp() {
+  atlasFollowUpPreviousRenderSteps();
+  requestAnimationFrame(atlasSyncRulesHeightToEditor);
+};
+
+function atlasMiniPickerResultsHtml() {
+  if (!visualPickerState) return '';
+  const options = visualPickerState.kind === 'source' ? pickerSourceOptions() : pickerFieldOptions(visualPickerState.sourceFile);
+  const query = visualPickerState.query || '';
+  const filtered = atlasSortedPickerItems(options.filter((option) => `${option.sourceFile} ${option.sourceAlias} ${option.fieldName} ${option.label}`.toLowerCase().includes(query.toLowerCase())));
+  const groups = new Map();
+  filtered.forEach((option) => {
+    const group = atlasFieldPickerSortMode === 'alpha' ? firstLetter(option.label || option.fieldName || option.sourceFile) : (visualPickerState.kind === 'source' ? 'Selected Sources' : option.sourceFile);
+    if (!groups.has(group)) groups.set(group, []);
+    groups.get(group).push(option);
+  });
+  return [...groups.entries()].map(([group, items]) => `<section class="visual-picker-group"><div class="visual-picker-group-header"><strong>${esc(String(group))}</strong><span>${items.length}</span></div><div class="visual-picker-pill-grid">${items.map((item) => `<button type="button" class="visual-picker-pill" data-mini-picker-value="${esc(visualPickerState.kind === 'source' ? item.sourceFile : item.fieldName)}" data-mini-picker-source="${esc(item.sourceFile)}" style="--pill-color:${esc(item.sourceColor || atlasSelectedSourceColor(item.sourceFile))};--source-color:${esc(item.sourceColor || atlasSelectedSourceColor(item.sourceFile))}"><span class="source-color-dot" style="background:${esc(item.sourceColor || atlasSelectedSourceColor(item.sourceFile))}"></span><span class="visual-picker-pill-main">${esc(visualPickerState.kind === 'source' ? item.sourceFile : item.label)}</span><span class="visual-picker-pill-detail">${esc(visualPickerState.kind === 'source' ? item.sourceAlias : `${item.sourceAlias}.${item.fieldName}`)}</span></button>`).join('')}</div></section>`).join('') || '<p class="hint visual-picker-empty">No selected source fields match the current search.</p>';
+}
+
+renderMiniPicker = function renderMiniPickerStableSearch() {
+  document.querySelector('.visual-picker-modal-backdrop.dynamic-mini-picker')?.remove();
+  if (!visualPickerState) return;
+  const root = document.createElement('div');
+  root.className = 'visual-picker-modal-backdrop dynamic-mini-picker';
+  root.innerHTML = `<div class="visual-picker-modal"><div class="visual-picker-modal-header"><div><h2 class="visual-picker-modal-title">${visualPickerState.kind === 'source' ? 'Choose Selected Source File' : 'Choose Field'}</h2><p class="visual-picker-modal-subtitle">Only sources selected for this audit are available here.</p></div><button type="button" class="visual-picker-close-button" data-close-mini-picker>×</button></div><div class="visual-picker-search-row"><input data-mini-picker-search value="${esc(visualPickerState.query || '')}" placeholder="Search selected sources and fields..."><div class="mini-picker-sort-toggle"><button type="button" class="secondary small ${atlasFieldPickerSortMode === 'source' ? 'active' : ''}" data-picker-sort="source">Sort by Source</button><button type="button" class="secondary small ${atlasFieldPickerSortMode === 'alpha' ? 'active' : ''}" data-picker-sort="alpha">A-Z</button></div></div><div class="visual-picker-content" data-mini-picker-results>${atlasMiniPickerResultsHtml()}</div></div>`;
+  document.body.append(root);
+  const input = root.querySelector('[data-mini-picker-search]');
+  input?.focus();
+  if (input) input.setSelectionRange(input.value.length, input.value.length);
+};
+
+function atlasRefreshMiniPickerResults() {
+  const results = document.querySelector('[data-mini-picker-results]');
+  if (results) results.innerHTML = atlasMiniPickerResultsHtml();
+}
+
+document.addEventListener('input', (event) => {
+  if (!event.target.matches('[data-mini-picker-search]') || !visualPickerState) return;
+  event.stopImmediatePropagation();
+  visualPickerState.query = event.target.value;
+  atlasRefreshMiniPickerResults();
+}, true);
+
+document.addEventListener('change', (event) => {
+  const valueMode = event.target.closest('[data-condition-field="valueMode"]');
+  if (valueMode) {
+    const row = valueMode.closest('.guided-condition-row');
+    const operator = row?.querySelector('[data-condition-field="operator"]');
+    if (operator) operator.innerHTML = atlasOperatorOptionsForValueMode(valueMode.value, operator.value);
+  }
+  if (event.target.closest('[data-param="then_action_type"]')) atlasCommitLiveEditor?.({ rerender: true });
+}, true);
+
+document.addEventListener('click', (event) => {
+  const createElse = event.target.closest('[data-create-else-step]');
+  if (createElse && !createElse.closest('.step-card')) {
+    const root = createElse.closest('[data-live-editor-step-id]');
+    const step = state.audit.steps.find((item) => item.id === root?.dataset.liveEditorStepId);
+    if (!step) return;
+    atlasCommitLiveEditor?.({ rerender: false });
+    normalizeCatalogStep(step);
+    step.params.include_else_branch = 'Yes';
+    const existing = state.audit.steps.find((candidate) => candidate.parentId === step.id && candidate.step_type === 'else');
+    if (!existing) {
+      state.audit.steps.push({ id: uid('step'), enabled: true, parentId: step.id, branchType: 'else', sortOrder: childrenOf(step.id).length + 1, title: 'ELSE branch', type: 'if', step_type: 'else', source: step.source, field: '', action: '', notes: '', conditions: [], params: { then_action_type: 'freeform', then_action: '' } });
+    }
+    renderAll();
+    scheduleAutosave();
+  }
+}, true);
+
+/* -----------------------------------------------------------------------------
+Source-aware Logic Builder token rendering
+----------------------------------------------------------------------------- */
+function atlasLogicSourceIdentity(sourceFile = '') {
+  const value = String(sourceFile || '').trim();
+  if (!value) return '';
+  const selected = (state.audit.sources || []).find((source) => {
+    const key = typeof sourceCatalogKey === 'function' ? sourceCatalogKey(source) : (source.sourceKey || source.name || source.sourceFile);
+    return value === key || value === source.name || value === source.sourceFile || value === source.alias;
+  });
+  return selected ? (selected.name || selected.sourceKey || selected.sourceFile || value) : value;
+}
+function atlasReferenceSourceForStep(step) {
+  let current = step;
+  const visited = new Set();
+  while (current && !visited.has(current.id)) {
+    visited.add(current.id);
+    const direct = current.params?.custom_field_source || current.params?.source_file || current.source || '';
+    if (direct) return atlasLogicSourceIdentity(direct);
+    current = current.parentId ? (state.audit.steps || []).find((candidate) => candidate.id === current.parentId) : null;
+  }
+  return '';
+}
+function atlasLogicTokenHtml(descriptor = {}) {
+  const label = String(descriptor.label || descriptor.fieldName || descriptor.sourceFile || '').trim();
+  if (!label) return '';
+  const sourceFile = atlasLogicSourceIdentity(descriptor.sourceFile || '');
+  const color = sourceFile ? atlasSelectedSourceColor(sourceFile) : '';
+  const kind = descriptor.kind || 'unresolved';
+  const classes = ['logic-pill', kind === 'source' ? 'source-pill' : kind === 'field' ? 'field-pill-token' : kind === 'custom' ? 'custom-field-token' : 'unresolved-field-token'];
+  const style = color ? [`--pill-bg:${color}`, `--pill-color:${color}`, `--source-color:${color}`, ...(kind === 'custom' ? [`--custom-field-color:${color}`] : [])].join(';') : '';
+  const identity = sourceFile && descriptor.fieldName ? `${sourceFile}.${descriptor.fieldName}` : (sourceFile || label);
+  return `<span class="${classes.join(' ')}"${style ? ` style="${esc(style)}"` : ''} title="${esc(identity)}" aria-label="${esc(identity)}">${esc(label)}</span>`;
+}
+function atlasLegacyLogicTokenHtml(value) {
+  const normalized = String(value || '').trim();
+  const sourceMatches = (state.audit.sources || []).filter((source) => {
+    const identity = source.name || source.sourceKey || source.sourceFile || '';
+    const alias = source.alias || (typeof atlasSelectedSourceAlias === 'function' ? atlasSelectedSourceAlias(identity) : '') || '';
+    return normalized === identity || normalized === source.sourceFile || normalized === alias;
+  }).map((source) => atlasLogicSourceIdentity(source.name || source.sourceKey || source.sourceFile));
+  const fieldMatches = [];
+  atlasSelectedAuditSources().forEach((source) => (source.fields || []).forEach((field) => {
+    const raw = field.fieldName || field.rawFieldName || field.name || field.label || '';
+    const vanity = field.vanityLabel || field.label || field.name || raw;
+    if (normalized === raw || normalized === vanity || normalized === `${source.sourceFile}.${raw}` || normalized === `${source.alias || source.sourceFile}.${raw}`) fieldMatches.push({ sourceFile: source.sourceFile, fieldName: raw });
+  }));
+  const uniqueSources = [...new Set(sourceMatches)];
+  const uniqueFields = [...new Map(fieldMatches.map((match) => [`${atlasLogicSourceIdentity(match.sourceFile)}::${match.fieldName}`, match])).values()];
+  if (uniqueSources.length === 1 && uniqueFields.length === 0) return atlasLogicTokenHtml({ kind: 'source', sourceFile: uniqueSources[0], label: normalized });
+  if (uniqueFields.length === 1 && uniqueSources.length === 0) return atlasLogicTokenHtml({ kind: 'field', sourceFile: uniqueFields[0].sourceFile, fieldName: uniqueFields[0].fieldName, label: normalized });
+  return atlasLogicTokenHtml({ kind: 'unresolved', label: normalized });
+}
+function atlasRenderLogicWithTokenDescriptors(text, descriptors = []) {
+  let index = 0;
+  return String(text || '').split(/("[^"]+")/g).map((part) => {
+    if (!part.startsWith('"') || !part.endsWith('"')) return esc(part);
+    const value = part.slice(1, -1);
+    const descriptor = descriptors[index++];
+    return descriptor ? atlasLogicTokenHtml({ ...descriptor, label: descriptor.label || value }) : atlasLegacyLogicTokenHtml(value);
+  }).join('');
+}
+function atlasStepLogicTokenDescriptors(step) {
+  normalizeCatalogStep?.(step);
+  const p = step?.params || {};
+  if (step?.step_type === 'reference_source') {
+    const sourceFile = p.source_file || step.source || '';
+    const alias = p.source_alias || sourceAliasFor(sourceFile) || sourceFile;
+    return alias && alias !== sourceFile ? [{ kind: 'source', sourceFile, label: sourceFile }, { kind: 'source', sourceFile, label: alias }] : [{ kind: 'source', sourceFile, label: sourceFile }];
+  }
+  if (step?.step_type !== 'create_column') return [];
+  const owner = atlasLogicSourceIdentity(p.custom_field_source || atlasReferenceSourceForStep(step) || p.source_file || step.source || '');
+  const descriptors = [{ kind: 'custom', sourceFile: owner, fieldName: p.column_name || step.field || '', label: p.column_name || step.field || '' }];
+  if (p.value_mode === 'Concatenate fields') {
+    const firstSource = atlasLogicSourceIdentity(p.concat_source_1 || p.source_file || owner);
+    const secondSource = atlasLogicSourceIdentity(p.concat_source_2 || p.source_file || owner);
+    descriptors.push(
+      { kind: 'source', sourceFile: firstSource, label: sourceAliasFor(firstSource) || firstSource },
+      { kind: 'field', sourceFile: firstSource, fieldName: p.concat_field_1, label: p.concat_field_1 },
+      { kind: 'source', sourceFile: secondSource, label: sourceAliasFor(secondSource) || secondSource },
+      { kind: 'field', sourceFile: secondSource, fieldName: p.concat_field_2, label: p.concat_field_2 }
+    );
+  } else if (p.value_mode === 'Source field value') {
+    const sourceFile = atlasLogicSourceIdentity(p.source_file || owner);
+    descriptors.push({ kind: 'source', sourceFile, label: sourceAliasFor(sourceFile) || sourceFile }, { kind: 'field', sourceFile, fieldName: p.field_name, label: p.field_name });
+  }
+  return descriptors;
+}
+function atlasRenderStepLogicWithPills(step) {
+  const text = analystLogic(step);
+  const descriptors = atlasStepLogicTokenDescriptors(step);
+  return descriptors.length ? atlasRenderLogicWithTokenDescriptors(text, descriptors) : renderLogicWithPills(text);
+}
+
